   421	    fun restoreIntroSnapshot(snapshot: CinerificIntroSnapshot) {
   422	        if (appliedIntroSnapshot == snapshot) return
   423	
   424	        val restoredFlow = enumValueOrNull<MockAccountFlow>(snapshot.activeFlowName)
   425	        val restoredRecoveryTarget = enumValueOrNull<MockForgotRecoveryTarget>(
   426	            snapshot.forgotRecoveryTargetName
   427	        ) ?: MockForgotRecoveryTarget.Password
   428	        val restoredSubmissionState = enumValueOrNull<MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState>(
   429	            snapshot.forgotPasswordSubmissionStateName
   430	        ) ?: MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Idle
   431	
   432	        pressedAvatarProfile = null
   433	        clickAvatarProfile = null
   434	        pressedCreateAccountPrompt = false
   435	        pressedSignInPrompt = false
   436	        pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = false
   437	        pressedMockField = null
   438	        pressedRememberMe = false
   439	        pressedBackChevron = false
   440	        pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
   441	        activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = false
   442	        mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
   443	        mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX = 0f
   444	        pressedMockDropdown = null
   445	        pressedMockDropdownOption = null
   446	        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = null
   447	        pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = false
   448	        activeMockDropdownScroll = null
   449	        mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
   450	        mockDragReturnInProgress = false
   451	        activeComposingText = ""
   452	        expandedMockDropdown = null
   453	        focusedMockField = null
   454	        mockInputDimAwaitingField = null
   455	        mockInputDimActiveField = null
   456	        mockInputDimActiveDropdown = null
   457	
   458	        activeMockFlow = restoredFlow
   459	        if (restoredFlow == null) {
   460	            mockSignInStartMillis = null
   461	            mockSignInTransitionStartProgress = 0f
   462	            mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress = 0f
   463	        } else {
   464	            mockSignInStartMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
   465	            mockSignInTransitionStartProgress = 1f
   466	            mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress = 1f
   467	        }
   468	
   469	        rememberMeChecked = snapshot.rememberMeChecked
   470	        usernameText = snapshot.usernameText
   471	        passwordText = snapshot.passwordText
   472	        confirmPasswordText = snapshot.confirmPasswordText
   473	        emailText = snapshot.emailText
   474	        monthText = snapshot.monthText
   475	        dayText = snapshot.dayText
   476	        yearText = snapshot.yearText
   477	        subscriptionTierText = normalizedMembershipTierSelection(snapshot.subscriptionTierText)
   478	        forgotRecoveryTarget = restoredRecoveryTarget
   479	        forgotPasswordSubmissionState = restoredSubmissionState
   480	        forgotPasswordSubmissionStartMillis = if (
   481	            restoredSubmissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Loading
   482	        ) {
   483	            SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
   484	        } else {
   485	            null
   486	        }
   487	
   488	        mockCreateAvatarIndex = snapshot.createAvatarIndex.coerceIn(0, MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_COUNT - 1)
   489	        portraitProfileIndex = snapshot.portraitProfileIndex.coerceIn(0, FINAL_AVATAR_TARGETS.lastIndex)
   490	        mockCreateAvatarCarouselStartMillis = null
   491	        mockCreateAvatarCarouselFromIndex = mockCreateAvatarIndex
   492	        mockCreateAvatarCarouselToIndex = mockCreateAvatarIndex
   493	        mockCreateAvatarCarouselDirection = MockAvatarCarouselDirection.Next
   494	        mockCreateAvatarCarouselStartProgress = 0f
   495	        mockDropdownScrollOffsets.keys.forEach { dropdown ->
   496	            mockDropdownScrollOffsets[dropdown] = 0
   497	        }
   498	
   499	        restartMockFormLabelAnimation()
   500	        restartMockFormFocusAnimation()
   501	        restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
   502	        restartMockInputDimAnimation()
   503	        appliedIntroSnapshot = currentIntroSnapshot()
   504	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   505	    }
   506	
   507	    override fun onSizeChanged(width: Int, height: Int, oldWidth: Int, oldHeight: Int) {
   508	        super.onSizeChanged(width, height, oldWidth, oldHeight)
   509	        pressedAvatarProfile = null
   510	        clickAvatarProfile = null
   511	        pressedCreateAccountPrompt = false
   512	        pressedSignInPrompt = false
   513	        pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = false
   514	        pressedMockField = null
   515	        pressedRememberMe = false
   516	        pressedBackChevron = false
   517	        pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
   518	        activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = false
   519	        mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
   520	        pressedMockDropdown = null
   521	        pressedMockDropdownOption = null
   522	        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = null
   523	        pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = false
   524	        activeMockDropdownScroll = null
   525	        mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
   526	        mockDragReturnInProgress = false
   527	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   528	    }
   529	
   530	    override fun onApplyWindowInsets(insets: WindowInsets): WindowInsets {
   531	        val systemBars = WindowInsetsCompat.toWindowInsetsCompat(insets, this)
   532	            .getInsets(WindowInsetsCompat.Type.systemBars())
   533	        systemBarInsetTopPx = systemBars.top
   534	        systemBarInsetBottomPx = systemBars.bottom
   535	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   536	        return super.onApplyWindowInsets(insets)
   537	    }
   538	
   539	    override fun onDraw(canvas: Canvas) {
   540	        super.onDraw(canvas)
   541	
   542	        val progress = bootProgressAt(bootStartMillis)
   543	        val blackToPurple = linearSegmentMs(
   544	            progress,
   545	            SCREEN_BLACK_HOLD_MS,
   546	            SCREEN_BLACK_HOLD_MS + SCREEN_PINK_MS
   547	        )
   548	        val purpleToSettle = easedSegmentMs(progress, 2500, 3900)
   549	        drawIntroBackground(canvas, blackToPurple, purpleToSettle)
   550	
   551	        val stage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return
   552	        finishMockSignInTransitionIfNeeded()
   553	        finishMockFlowSwitchIfNeeded()
   554	        finishMockCreateAvatarCarouselIfNeeded()
   555	        finishMockForgotPasswordSubmissionIfNeeded()
   556	        val mockProgress = mockSignInProgress()
   557	        updateMockFormFocusAnimation()
   558	        updateMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
   559	        updateMockInputDimAnimation()
   560	        val formFocusMotion = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFormFocusProgress)
   561	
   562	        val logoAlpha = linearSegmentMs(progress, 90, LOGO_ENTRY_START_MS)
   563	        if (logoAlpha > 0.01f) {
   564	            drawSettlingLogo(canvas, stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, progress, logoAlpha, formFocusMotion)
   565	        }
   566	
   567	        val avatarAlpha = easedSegmentMs(progress, 2300, FINAL_SETTLE_END_MS)
   568	        val mockMotion = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockProgress)
   569	        if (avatarAlpha > 0.01f) {
   570	            val stackAlpha = avatarAlpha * (1f - mockMotion)
   571	            val y = lerpFloat(56f, 0f, avatarAlpha) + MOCK_SIGN_IN_STACK_EXIT_Y * mockMotion
   572	            val stackShiftY = signInStackShiftY()
   573	            if (stackAlpha > 0.01f) {
   574	                if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
   575	                    drawPortraitProfile(canvas, stage, y, stackAlpha)
   576	                } else {
   577	                    drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, steveAvatar, Bounds(152f, 450f + stackShiftY + y, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, stackAlpha)
   578	                    drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, martinAvatar, Bounds(390f, 450f + stackShiftY + y, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, stackAlpha)
   579	                    drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, jannyAvatar, Bounds(628f, 454f + stackShiftY + y, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, stackAlpha)
   580	                    drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, guestAvatar, Bounds(866f, 450f + stackShiftY + y, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, stackAlpha)
   581	
   582	                    drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, steveName, Bounds(130f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + y, 220f, 72f), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, stackAlpha)
   583	                    drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, martinName, Bounds(368f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + y, 220f, 72f), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, stackAlpha)
   584	                    drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, jannyName, Bounds(606f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + y, 220f, 72f), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, stackAlpha)
   585	                    drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, guestName, Bounds(854f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + y, 200f, 72f), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, stackAlpha)
   586	                }
   587	            }
   588	        }
   589	        if (mockSignInStartMillis != null) {
   590	            val outgoingFlow = outgoingMockFlow
   591	            if (outgoingFlow != null) {
   592	                val switchMotion = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFlowSwitchProgress())
   593	                drawMockSignInFormForFlow(
   594	                    canvas,
   595	                    outgoingFlow,
   596	                    stage.left,
   597	                    stage.top,
   598	                    stage.scale,
   599	                    1f - switchMotion,
   600	                    formFocusMotion,
   601	                    updateLabelAnimation = false
   602	                )
   603	                activeMockFlow?.let { incomingFlow ->
   604	                    drawMockSignInFormForFlow(
   605	                        canvas,
   606	                        incomingFlow,
   607	                        stage.left,
   608	                        stage.top,
   609	                        stage.scale,
   610	                        switchMotion,
   611	                        formFocusMotion,
   612	                        updateLabelAnimation = true
   613	                    )
   614	                }
   615	            } else {
   616	                drawMockSignInForm(canvas, stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, mockMotion, formFocusMotion)
   617	            }
   618	            drawBackChevron(
   619	                canvas,
   620	                ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_CENTER_X,
   621	                mockBackChevronRowTopY(),
   622	                stage.left,
   623	                accountPromptStageTop(stage.scale),
   624	                stage.scale,
   625	                mockMotion
   626	            )
   627	        }
   628	        drawAccountPrompt(
   629	            canvas = canvas,
   630	            stageLeft = stage.left,
   631	            stageTop = if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) stage.top else accountPromptStageTop(stage.scale),
   632	            stageScale = stage.scale,
   633	            yOffset = if (mockSignInStartMillis == null) {
   634	                lerpFloat(56f, 0f, avatarAlpha)
   635	            } else {
   636	                0f
   637	            },
   638	            alpha = avatarAlpha
   639	        )
   640	
   641	        if (
   642	            progress < 1f ||
   643	            isMockSignInAnimating() ||
   644	            isMockFlowSwitchAnimating() ||
   645	            isMockFormLabelAnimating() ||
   646	            isMockFormFocusAnimating() ||
   647	            isMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimating() ||
   648	            isMockCreateAvatarCarouselAnimating() ||
   649	            isMockForgotPasswordSubmissionAnimating() ||
   650	            isMockInputDimAnimating()
   651	        ) {
   652	            postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   653	        }
   654	    }
   655	
   656	    override fun onTouchEvent(event: MotionEvent): Boolean {
   657	        return when (event.actionMasked) {
   658	            MotionEvent.ACTION_DOWN -> {
   659	                mockTouchDownX = event.x
   660	                mockTouchDownY = event.y
   661	                portraitProfileSwipeMoved = false
   662	                if (mockSignInStartMillis != null) {
   663	                    mockDragReturnInProgress = false
   664	                    mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
   665	                    if (isMockFlowSwitchAnimating()) {
   666	                        return true
   667	                    }
   668	                    pressedCreateAccountPrompt = settledCreateAccountPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
   669	                    pressedSignInPrompt = !pressedCreateAccountPrompt &&
   670	                        settledSignInPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
   671	                    pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = !pressedCreateAccountPrompt &&
   672	                        !pressedSignInPrompt &&
   673	                        settledForgotPasswordPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
   674	                    if (
   675	                        pressedCreateAccountPrompt ||
   676	                        pressedSignInPrompt ||
   677	                        pressedForgotPasswordPrompt
   678	                    ) {
   679	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   680	                        return true
   681	                    }
   682	                    val touchedDropdownOptions = mockDropdownOptionsHit(event.x, event.y)
   683	                    activeMockDropdownScroll = touchedDropdownOptions?.takeIf { mockDropdownCanScroll(it) }
   684	                    mockDropdownScrollStartY = event.y
   685	                    mockDropdownScrollStartOffset = activeMockDropdownScroll?.let { mockDropdownScrollOffset(it) } ?: 0
   686	                    pressedMockDropdownOption = if (touchedDropdownOptions != null) {
   687	                        mockDropdownOptionHit(event.x, event.y)
   688	                    } else {
   689	                        null
   690	                    }
   691	                    pressedMockDropdown = if (pressedMockDropdownOption == null && touchedDropdownOptions == null) {
   692	                        mockDropdownHit(event.x, event.y)
   693	                    } else {
   694	                        null
   695	                    }
   696	                    pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = if (
   697	                        touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   698	                        pressedMockDropdownOption == null &&
   699	                        pressedMockDropdown == null
   700	                    ) {
   701	                        mockForgotRecoveryTargetHit(event.x, event.y)
   702	                    } else {
   703	                        null
   704	                    }
   705	                    pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   706	                        pressedMockDropdownOption == null &&
   707	                        pressedMockDropdown == null &&
   708	                        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget == null &&
   709	                        mockForgotPasswordSubmitHit(event.x, event.y)
   710	                    val touchedCreateAvatarCarousel = touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   711	                        pressedMockDropdownOption == null &&
   712	                        pressedMockDropdown == null &&
   713	                        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget == null &&
   714	                        !pressedForgotPasswordSubmit &&
   715	                        mockCreateAvatarCarouselHit(event.x, event.y) &&
   716	                        !isMockCreateAvatarCarouselAnimating()
   717	                    activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = touchedCreateAvatarCarousel
   718	                    mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
   719	                    mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX = 0f
   720	                    pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = if (
   721	                        touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   722	                        pressedMockDropdownOption == null &&
   723	                        pressedMockDropdown == null &&
   724	                        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget == null &&
   725	                        !pressedForgotPasswordSubmit &&
   726	                        touchedCreateAvatarCarousel
   727	                    ) {
   728	                        mockCreateAvatarNavHit(event.x, event.y)
   729	                    } else {
   730	                        null
   731	                    }
   732	                    pressedMockField = if (
   733	                        touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   734	                        pressedMockDropdownOption == null &&
   735	                        pressedMockDropdown == null &&
   736	                        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget == null &&
   737	                        pressedMockCreateAvatarNav == null &&
   738	                        !pressedForgotPasswordSubmit &&
   739	                        !touchedCreateAvatarCarousel
   740	                    ) {
   741	                        mockSignInFieldHit(event.x, event.y)
   742	                    } else {
   743	                        null
   744	                    }
   745	                    pressedRememberMe = touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   746	                        !touchedCreateAvatarCarousel &&
   747	                        !pressedForgotPasswordSubmit &&
   748	                        pressedMockField == null &&
   749	                        mockRememberMeHit(event.x, event.y)
   750	                    pressedBackChevron = pressedMockField == null &&
   751	                        touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   752	                        !touchedCreateAvatarCarousel &&
   753	                        !pressedRememberMe &&
   754	                        pressedMockDropdown == null &&
   755	                        pressedMockDropdownOption == null &&
   756	                        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget == null &&
   757	                        pressedMockCreateAvatarNav == null &&
   758	                        !pressedForgotPasswordSubmit &&
   759	                        mockBackChevronHit(event.x, event.y)
   760	                    postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   761	                    return true
   762	                }
   763	                pressedCreateAccountPrompt = settledCreateAccountPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
   764	                pressedSignInPrompt = !pressedCreateAccountPrompt && settledSignInPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
   765	                pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = !pressedCreateAccountPrompt &&
   766	                    !pressedSignInPrompt &&
   767	                    settledForgotPasswordPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
   768	                pressedAvatarProfile = if (
   769	                    pressedCreateAccountPrompt ||
   770	                    pressedSignInPrompt ||
   771	                    pressedForgotPasswordPrompt
   772	                ) {
   773	                    null
   774	                } else {
   775	                    settledAvatarHitProfile(event.x, event.y)
   776	                }
   777	                pressedCreateAccountPrompt ||
   778	                    pressedSignInPrompt ||
   779	                    pressedForgotPasswordPrompt ||
   780	                    pressedAvatarProfile != null
   781	            }
   782	            MotionEvent.ACTION_MOVE -> {
   783	                if (mockSignInStartMillis != null) {
   784	                    if (updateMockCreateAvatarDrag(event)) {
   785	                        return true
   786	                    }
   787	                    if (updateMockDropdownScroll(event)) {
   788	                        return true
   789	                    }
   790	                    if (mockDropdownOptionsHit(mockTouchDownX, mockTouchDownY) == null && shouldStartMockDragReturn(event)) {
   791	                        mockDragReturnInProgress = true
   792	                        closeMockSignInScreen()
   793	                    }
   794	                    return true
   795	                }
   796	                if (isPortraitIntroLayout() && pressedAvatarProfile != null) {
   797	                    val horizontalDelta = event.x - mockTouchDownX
   798	                    val verticalDelta = event.y - mockTouchDownY
   799	                    if (abs(horizontalDelta) > touchSlop && abs(horizontalDelta) > abs(verticalDelta)) {
   800	                        portraitProfileSwipeMoved = true
   801	                    }
   802	                    return true
   803	                }
   804	                pressedCreateAccountPrompt ||
   805	                    pressedSignInPrompt ||
   806	                    pressedForgotPasswordPrompt ||
   807	                    pressedAvatarProfile != null
   808	            }
   809	            MotionEvent.ACTION_UP -> {
   810	                if (mockDragReturnInProgress) {
   811	                    mockDragReturnInProgress = false
   812	                    pressedMockField = null
   813	                    pressedRememberMe = false
   814	                    pressedBackChevron = false
   815	                    pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
   816	                    activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = false
   817	                    mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
   818	                    mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX = 0f
   819	                    pressedMockDropdown = null
   820	                    pressedMockDropdownOption = null
   821	                    pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = null
   822	                    pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = false
   823	                    activeMockDropdownScroll = null
   824	                    mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
   825	                    return true
   826	                }
   827	                if (mockSignInStartMillis != null) {
   828	                    if (isMockFlowSwitchAnimating()) {
   829	                        pressedCreateAccountPrompt = false
   830	                        pressedSignInPrompt = false
   831	                        pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = false
   832	                        return true
   833	                    }
   834	                    val requestedFlow = when {
   835	                        pressedCreateAccountPrompt && settledCreateAccountPromptHit(event.x, event.y) -> {
   836	                            MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount
   837	                        }
   838	                        pressedSignInPrompt && settledSignInPromptHit(event.x, event.y) -> {
   839	                            MockAccountFlow.SignIn
   840	                        }
   841	                        pressedForgotPasswordPrompt && settledForgotPasswordPromptHit(event.x, event.y) -> {
   842	                            MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword
   843	                        }
   844	                        else -> null
   845	                    }
   846	                    pressedCreateAccountPrompt = false
   847	                    pressedSignInPrompt = false
   848	                    pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = false
   849	                    if (requestedFlow != null) {
   850	                        super.performClick()
   851	                        switchMockAccountFlow(requestedFlow)
   852	                        return true
   853	                    }
   854	                    val releasedDropdownOption = mockDropdownOptionHit(event.x, event.y)
   855	                    val releasedDropdown = mockDropdownHit(event.x, event.y)
   856	                    val releasedField = mockSignInFieldHit(event.x, event.y)
   857	                    val releasedRememberMe = mockRememberMeHit(event.x, event.y)
   858	                    val releasedBackChevron = mockBackChevronHit(event.x, event.y)
   859	                    val releasedCreateAvatarNav = mockCreateAvatarNavHit(event.x, event.y)
   860	                    val releasedForgotRecoveryTarget = mockForgotRecoveryTargetHit(event.x, event.y)
   861	                    val releasedForgotPasswordSubmit = mockForgotPasswordSubmitHit(event.x, event.y)
   862	                    val wasTouchingDropdownOptions = activeMockDropdownScroll != null ||
   863	                        mockDropdownOptionsHit(mockTouchDownX, mockTouchDownY) != null
   864	                    val wasScrollingDropdown = mockDropdownScrollMoved
   865	                    val wasPressingRememberMe = pressedRememberMe
   866	                    val wasPressingBackChevron = pressedBackChevron
   867	                    val wasPressingForgotRecoveryTarget = pressedForgotRecoveryTarget != null
   868	                    val wasPressingForgotPasswordSubmit = pressedForgotPasswordSubmit
   869	                    val wasDraggingCreateAvatar = activeMockCreateAvatarDrag
   870	                    val createAvatarDragMoved = mockCreateAvatarDragMoved
   871	                    val createAvatarDragDirection = mockCreateAvatarDragDirection()
   872	                    val createAvatarDragProgress = mockCreateAvatarDragProgress()
   873	                    val pressedDropdown = pressedMockDropdown
   874	                    val pressedOption = pressedMockDropdownOption
   875	                    val pressedCreateAvatarNav = pressedMockCreateAvatarNav
   876	                    val pressedForgotRecoverySelection = pressedForgotRecoveryTarget
   877	                    val shouldSelectDropdownOption = !wasScrollingDropdown &&
   878	                        pressedOption != null &&
   879	                        pressedOption == releasedDropdownOption
   880	                    val shouldSelectForgotRecoveryTarget = pressedForgotRecoverySelection != null &&
   881	                        pressedForgotRecoverySelection == releasedForgotRecoveryTarget
   882	                    val shouldToggleDropdown = pressedDropdown != null && pressedDropdown == releasedDropdown
   883	                    val shouldToggleRememberMe = wasPressingRememberMe && releasedRememberMe
   884	                    val shouldCloseMockSignIn = wasPressingBackChevron && releasedBackChevron
   885	                    val shouldSubmitForgotPassword = wasPressingForgotPasswordSubmit && releasedForgotPasswordSubmit
   886	                    val shouldNavigateCreateAvatar = pressedCreateAvatarNav != null &&
   887	                        !createAvatarDragMoved &&
   888	                        pressedCreateAvatarNav == releasedCreateAvatarNav
   889	                    val shouldSwipeCreateAvatar = wasDraggingCreateAvatar &&
   890	                        createAvatarDragMoved &&
   891	                        createAvatarDragDirection != null &&
   892	                        createAvatarDragProgress >= MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_DRAG_COMMIT_PROGRESS
   893	                    val shouldFocusField = releasedField != null &&
   894	                        !wasTouchingDropdownOptions &&
   895	                        !wasPressingRememberMe &&
   896	                        !wasPressingBackChevron &&
   897	                        !wasPressingForgotRecoveryTarget &&
   898	                        !wasPressingForgotPasswordSubmit &&
   899	                        !wasDraggingCreateAvatar &&
   900	                        pressedDropdown == null &&
   901	                        pressedOption == null &&
   902	                        pressedCreateAvatarNav == null &&
   903	                        (pressedMockField == null || pressedMockField == releasedField)
   904	                    pressedMockField = null
   905	                    pressedRememberMe = false
   906	                    pressedBackChevron = false
   907	                    pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
   908	                    activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = false
   909	                    mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
   910	                    mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX = 0f
   911	                    pressedMockDropdown = null
   912	                    pressedMockDropdownOption = null
   913	                    this.pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = null
   914	                    pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = false
   915	                    activeMockDropdownScroll = null
   916	                    mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
   917	                    super.performClick()
   918	                    if (shouldCloseMockSignIn) {
   919	                        closeMockSignInScreen()
   920	                    } else if (shouldSwipeCreateAvatar && createAvatarDragDirection != null) {
   921	                        expandedMockDropdown = null
   922	                        clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
   923	                        startMockCreateAvatarCarousel(createAvatarDragDirection, createAvatarDragProgress)
   924	                    } else if (shouldNavigateCreateAvatar && pressedCreateAvatarNav != null) {
   925	                        expandedMockDropdown = null
   926	                        clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
   927	                        startMockCreateAvatarCarousel(pressedCreateAvatarNav)
   928	                    } else if (wasDraggingCreateAvatar) {
   929	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   930	                    } else if (shouldSelectDropdownOption && pressedOption != null) {
   931	                        setMockDropdownValue(pressedOption)
   932	                        expandedMockDropdown = null
   933	                        restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
   934	                        restartMockInputDimAnimation()
   935	                        clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
   936	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   937	                    } else if (shouldToggleDropdown && pressedDropdown != null) {
   938	                        expandedMockDropdown = if (expandedMockDropdown == pressedDropdown) null else pressedDropdown
   939	                        expandedMockDropdown?.let { prepareMockDropdownScroll(it) }
   940	                        restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
   941	                        restartMockInputDimAnimation()
   942	                        clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
   943	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   944	                    } else if (shouldToggleRememberMe) {
   945	                        rememberMeChecked = !rememberMeChecked
   946	                        notifyIntroSnapshotChanged()
   947	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   948	                    } else if (shouldSelectForgotRecoveryTarget && pressedForgotRecoverySelection != null) {
   949	                        setMockForgotRecoveryTarget(pressedForgotRecoverySelection)
   950	                    } else if (shouldSubmitForgotPassword) {
   951	                        startMockForgotPasswordSubmission()
   952	                    } else if (shouldFocusField && releasedField != null) {
   953	                        expandedMockDropdown = null
   954	                        restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
   955	                        restartMockInputDimAnimation()
   956	                        focusMockSignInField(releasedField)
   957	                    } else if (wasTouchingDropdownOptions || wasScrollingDropdown) {
   958	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   959	                    } else if (!wasPressingRememberMe && !wasPressingBackChevron && !wasPressingForgotRecoveryTarget) {
   960	                        if (expandedMockDropdown != null && activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount) {
   961	                            expandedMockDropdown = null
   962	                            restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
   963	                            restartMockInputDimAnimation()
   964	                        }
   965	                        clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
   966	                    } else {
   967	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   968	                    }
   969	                    return true
   970	                }
   971	                val shouldOpenMockCreateAccount = pressedCreateAccountPrompt &&
   972	                    settledCreateAccountPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
   973	                val shouldOpenMockSignIn = pressedSignInPrompt && settledSignInPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
   974	                val shouldOpenMockForgotPassword = pressedForgotPasswordPrompt &&
   975	                    settledForgotPasswordPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
   976	                val releasedAvatarProfile = settledAvatarHitProfile(event.x, event.y)
   977	                val swipeDeltaX = event.x - mockTouchDownX
   978	                val swipeThreshold = PORTRAIT_PROFILE_SWIPE_COMMIT_DP * resources.displayMetrics.density
   979	                val shouldSwipeProfile = isPortraitIntroLayout() &&
   980	                    pressedAvatarProfile != null &&
   981	                    portraitProfileSwipeMoved &&
   982	                    abs(swipeDeltaX) >= swipeThreshold
   983	                val shouldNavigate = !shouldSwipeProfile && !portraitProfileSwipeMoved &&
   984	                    pressedAvatarProfile != null && pressedAvatarProfile == releasedAvatarProfile
   985	                clickAvatarProfile = pressedAvatarProfile
   986	                pressedAvatarProfile = null
   987	                pressedCreateAccountPrompt = false
   988	                pressedSignInPrompt = false
   989	                pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = false
   990	                if (shouldSwipeProfile) {
   991	                    portraitProfileIndex = if (swipeDeltaX < 0f) {
   992	                        (portraitProfileIndex + 1) % FINAL_AVATAR_TARGETS.size
   993	                    } else {
   994	                        (portraitProfileIndex - 1 + FINAL_AVATAR_TARGETS.size) % FINAL_AVATAR_TARGETS.size
   995	                    }
   996	                    clickAvatarProfile = null
   997	                    portraitProfileSwipeMoved = false
   998	                    notifyIntroSnapshotChanged()
   999	                    postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  1000	                    true
  1001	                } else if (shouldOpenMockCreateAccount) {
  1002	                    super.performClick()
  1003	                    openMockCreateAccountScreen()
  1004	                    true
  1005	                } else if (shouldOpenMockSignIn) {
  1006	                    super.performClick()
  1007	                    openMockSignInScreen()
  1008	                    true
  1009	                } else if (shouldOpenMockForgotPassword) {
  1010	                    super.performClick()
  1011	                    openMockForgotPasswordScreen()
  1012	                    true
  1013	                } else if (shouldNavigate) {
  1014	                    performClick()
  1015	                    true
  1016	                } else {
  1017	                    clickAvatarProfile = null
  1018	                    false
  1019	                }
  1020	            }
  1021	            MotionEvent.ACTION_CANCEL -> {
  1022	                pressedAvatarProfile = null
  1023	                clickAvatarProfile = null
  1024	                pressedCreateAccountPrompt = false
  1025	                pressedSignInPrompt = false
  1026	                pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = false
  1027	                pressedMockField = null
  1028	                pressedRememberMe = false
  1029	                pressedBackChevron = false
  1030	                pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
  1031	                activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = false
  1032	                mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
  1033	                mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX = 0f
  1034	                pressedMockDropdown = null
  1035	                pressedMockDropdownOption = null
  1036	                pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = null
  1037	                pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = false
  1038	                activeMockDropdownScroll = null
  1039	                mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
  1040	                mockDragReturnInProgress = false
  1041	                portraitProfileSwipeMoved = false
  1042	                false
  1043	            }
  1044	            else -> pressedCreateAccountPrompt ||
  1045	                pressedSignInPrompt ||
  1046	                pressedForgotPasswordPrompt ||
  1047	                pressedAvatarProfile != null ||
  1048	                mockSignInStartMillis != null
  1049	        }
  1050	    }
  1051	
  1052	    override fun onCheckIsTextEditor(): Boolean {
  1053	        return mockSignInStartMillis != null && !isMockSignInClosing()
  1054	    }
  1055	
  1056	    override fun onCreateInputConnection(outAttrs: EditorInfo): InputConnection? {
  1057	        val field = focusedMockField ?: return null
  1058	        outAttrs.inputType = when (field) {
  1059	            MockSignInField.Password,
  1060	            MockSignInField.ConfirmPassword -> InputType.TYPE_CLASS_TEXT or InputType.TYPE_TEXT_VARIATION_PASSWORD
  1061	            MockSignInField.Email -> InputType.TYPE_CLASS_TEXT or InputType.TYPE_TEXT_VARIATION_EMAIL_ADDRESS
  1062	            MockSignInField.Month,
  1063	            MockSignInField.Day,
  1064	            MockSignInField.Year -> InputType.TYPE_CLASS_NUMBER
  1065	            else -> InputType.TYPE_CLASS_TEXT or InputType.TYPE_TEXT_VARIATION_NORMAL
  1066	        }
  1067	        outAttrs.imeOptions = if (field == activeMockFields().lastOrNull()) {
  1068	            EditorInfo.IME_ACTION_DONE
  1069	        } else {
  1070	            EditorInfo.IME_ACTION_NEXT
  1071	        }
  1072	        val textLength = mockSignInFieldText(field).length
  1073	        outAttrs.initialSelStart = textLength
  1074	        outAttrs.initialSelEnd = textLength
  1075	        return MockSignInInputConnection()
  1076	    }
  1077	
  1078	    override fun onKeyDown(keyCode: Int, event: KeyEvent): Boolean {
  1079	        if (focusedMockField != null) {
  1080	            return handleMockSignInKeyEvent(keyCode, event) || super.onKeyDown(keyCode, event)
  1081	        }
  1082	        return super.onKeyDown(keyCode, event)
  1083	    }
  1084	
  1085	    @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
  1086	    override fun onKeyMultiple(keyCode: Int, repeatCount: Int, event: KeyEvent): Boolean {
  1087	        val characters = event.getCharacters()
  1088	        if (focusedMockField != null && !characters.isNullOrEmpty()) {
  1089	            return appendMockSignInInput(characters)
  1090	        }
  1091	        return super.onKeyMultiple(keyCode, repeatCount, event)
  1092	    }
  1093	
  1094	    override fun performClick(): Boolean {
  1095	        super.performClick()
  1096	        if (!isFinalFrameSettled()) return false
  1097	        val profile = clickAvatarProfile ?: return false
  1098	        onAvatarSelected?.invoke(profile)
  1099	        clickAvatarProfile = null
  1100	        return true
  1101	    }
  1102	
  1103	    private fun drawSettlingLogo(
  1104	        canvas: Canvas,
  1105	        stageLeft: Float,
  1106	        stageTop: Float,
  1107	        stageScale: Float,
  1108	        progress: Float,
  1109	        alpha: Float,
  1110	        formFocusMotion: Float
  1111	    ) {
  1112	        val logoEntry = bouncySegmentMs(progress, LOGO_ENTRY_START_MS, LOGO_ENTRY_END_MS)
  1113	        val finalProgress = easedSegmentMs(progress, FINAL_SETTLE_START_MS, FINAL_SETTLE_END_MS)
  1114	        val focusShiftY = mockLogoFocusShiftY(formFocusMotion)
  1115	        val portraitAmount = portraitIntroAmount()
  1116	        val portraitLogo = Bounds(
  1117	            (FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH - 600f * PORTRAIT_INTRO_SCALE) / 2f +
  1118	                PORTRAIT_LOGO_VISIBLE_CENTER_OFFSET_X,
  1119	            portraitLogoTop(),
  1120	            600f * PORTRAIT_INTRO_SCALE,
  1121	            LOGO_FINAL_HEIGHT * PORTRAIT_INTRO_SCALE
  1122	        )
  1123	        val base = offsetBounds(
  1124	            lerpBounds(Bounds(222f, 150f, 750f, 535f), portraitLogo, portraitAmount),
  1125	            focusShiftY
  1126	        )
  1127	        val final = offsetBounds(
  1128	            lerpBounds(Bounds(297f, LOGO_FINAL_TOP, 600f, LOGO_FINAL_HEIGHT), portraitLogo, portraitAmount),
  1129	            focusShiftY
  1130	        )
  1131	
  1132	        if (logoEntry >= 0.999f) {
  1133	            drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, logoCombined, lerpBounds(base, final, finalProgress), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
  1134	            return
  1135	        }
  1136	
  1137	        val simpleEntry = lerpBounds(Bounds(447f, 1037f, 300f, 214f), base, logoEntry)
  1138	        val eyesEntry = lerpBounds(Bounds(453f, -417f, 300f, 214f), base, logoEntry)
  1139	        drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, logoSimple, lerpBounds(simpleEntry, final, finalProgress), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
  1140	        drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, logoEyes, lerpBounds(eyesEntry, final, finalProgress), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
  1141	    }

  1143	    private fun drawPortraitProfile(
  1144	        canvas: Canvas,
  1145	        stage: StageMetrics,
  1146	        yOffset: Float,
  1147	        alpha: Float
  1148	    ) {
  1149	        val profile = FINAL_AVATAR_TARGETS[portraitProfileIndex].profile
  1150	        val avatarBitmap = when (profile) {
  1151	            CinerificProfile.Steve -> steveAvatar
  1152	            CinerificProfile.Martin -> martinAvatar
  1153	            CinerificProfile.Janny -> jannyAvatar
  1154	            CinerificProfile.Guest -> guestAvatar
  1155	        }
  1156	        val nameBitmap = when (profile) {
  1157	            CinerificProfile.Steve -> steveName
  1158	            CinerificProfile.Martin -> martinName
  1159	            CinerificProfile.Janny -> jannyName
  1160	            CinerificProfile.Guest -> guestName
  1161	        }
  1162	        val avatarSize = portraitAvatarSize()
  1163	        val nameWidth = (if (profile == CinerificProfile.Guest) 200f else 220f) * PORTRAIT_PROFILE_SCALE
  1164	        drawFigmaBitmap(
  1165	            canvas,
  1166	            avatarBitmap,
  1167	            Bounds(
  1168	                (FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH - avatarSize) / 2f,
  1169	                portraitAvatarTop() + yOffset,
  1170	                avatarSize,
  1171	                avatarSize
  1172	            ),
  1173	            stage.left,
  1174	            stage.top,
  1175	            stage.scale,
  1176	            alpha
  1177	        )
  1178	        drawFigmaBitmap(
  1179	            canvas,
  1180	            nameBitmap,
  1181	            Bounds(
  1182	                (FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH - nameWidth) / 2f,
  1183	                portraitProfileNameTop() + yOffset,
  1184	                nameWidth,
  1185	                portraitProfileNameHeight()
  1186	            ),
  1187	            stage.left,
  1188	            stage.top,
  1189	            stage.scale,
  1190	            alpha
  1191	        )
  1192	    }
  1193	
  1194	    private fun drawIntroBackground(canvas: Canvas, solidProgress: Float, gradientProgress: Float) {
  1195	        val standardSignInBackground = standardSignInBackgroundForSize()
  1196	        if (gradientProgress <= 0.001f) {
  1197	            canvas.drawColor(lerpColor(COLOR_FRAME_1, COLOR_FRAME_2, solidProgress))
  1198	            return
  1199	        }
  1200	
  1201	        val solid = lerpColor(COLOR_FRAME_1, COLOR_FRAME_2, solidProgress)
  1202	        backgroundPaint.shader = LinearGradient(
  1203	            0f,
  1204	            0f,
  1205	            0f,
  1206	            height.toFloat(),
  1207	            intArrayOf(
  1208	                lerpColor(solid, COLOR_GRADIENT_TOP, gradientProgress),
  1209	                lerpColor(solid, COLOR_GRADIENT_CENTER, gradientProgress),
  1210	                lerpColor(solid, COLOR_GRADIENT_BOTTOM, gradientProgress)
  1211	            ),
  1212	            null,
  1213	            Shader.TileMode.CLAMP
  1214	        )
  1215	        canvas.drawRect(0f, 0f, width.toFloat(), height.toFloat(), backgroundPaint)
  1216	        backgroundPaint.shader = null
  1217	
  1218	        if (standardSignInBackground != null) {
  1219	            drawStandardSignInBlackMatte(canvas, gradientProgress)
  1220	            drawStandardSignInBackground(canvas, standardSignInBackground, gradientProgress)
  1221	        }
  1222	    }
  1223	
  1224	    private fun drawStandardSignInBlackMatte(canvas: Canvas, alpha: Float) {
  1225	        backgroundPaint.color = COLOR_FRAME_1
  1226	        backgroundPaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
  1227	        canvas.drawRect(0f, 0f, width.toFloat(), height.toFloat(), backgroundPaint)
  1228	        backgroundPaint.alpha = 255
  1229	    }
  1230	
  1231	    private fun drawStandardSignInBackground(canvas: Canvas, bitmap: Bitmap, alpha: Float) {
  1232	        tempRect.set(0f, 0f, width.toFloat(), height.toFloat())
  1233	        imagePaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * STANDARD_BACKGROUND_SETTLED_ALPHA * 255f).roundToInt()
  1234	        canvas.drawBitmap(bitmap, null, tempRect, imagePaint)
  1235	        imagePaint.alpha = 255
  1236	    }
  1237	
  1238	    private fun drawFigmaBitmap(
  1239	        canvas: Canvas,
  1240	        bitmap: Bitmap,
  1241	        bounds: Bounds,
  1242	        stageLeft: Float,
  1243	        stageTop: Float,
  1244	        stageScale: Float,
  1245	        alpha: Float
  1246	    ) {
  1247	        tempRect.set(
  1248	            stageLeft + bounds.x * stageScale,
  1249	            stageTop + bounds.y * stageScale,
  1250	            stageLeft + (bounds.x + bounds.w) * stageScale,
  1251	            stageTop + (bounds.y + bounds.h) * stageScale
  1252	        )
  1253	        imagePaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
  1254	        canvas.drawBitmap(bitmap, null, tempRect, imagePaint)
  1255	        imagePaint.alpha = 255
  1256	    }
  1257	
  1258	    private fun drawAccountPrompt(
  1259	        canvas: Canvas,
  1260	        stageLeft: Float,
  1261	        stageTop: Float,
  1262	        stageScale: Float,
  1263	        yOffset: Float,
  1264	        alpha: Float
  1265	    ) {
  1266	        val isLandscape = width > height
  1267	        val portraitAmount = portraitIntroAmount()
  1268	        val textSize = lerpFloat(
  1269	            signInAccountPromptTextSize(),
  1270	            ACCOUNT_PROMPT_TEXT_SIZE * PORTRAIT_INTRO_SCALE,
  1271	            portraitAmount
  1272	        ) * stageScale
  1273	        val paintAlpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
  1274	        accountPromptPaint.textSize = textSize
  1275	        formTitlePaint.textSize = textSize
  1276	        accountPromptPaint.alpha = paintAlpha
  1277	        formTitlePaint.alpha = paintAlpha
  1278	
  1279	        val accountPromptShiftY = if (isLandscape) SIGN_IN_LANDSCAPE_ACCOUNT_PROMPT_EXTRA_SHIFT_Y else 0f
  1280	        val standardCenterY = stageTop +
  1281	            (ACCOUNT_PROMPT_CENTER_Y + signInStackShiftY() + accountPromptShiftY + yOffset) * stageScale
  1282	        val secondaryMetrics = accountPromptPaint.fontMetrics
  1283	        val primaryMetrics = formTitlePaint.fontMetrics
  1284	        fun promptCenterY(portraitCenterY: Float): Float = lerpFloat(
  1285	            standardCenterY,
  1286	            stageTop + (portraitCenterY + yOffset) * stageScale,
  1287	            portraitAmount
  1288	        )
  1289	        val createCenterY = promptCenterY(portraitPromptCreateCenterY())
  1290	        val signInCenterY = promptCenterY(portraitPromptSignInCenterY())
  1291	        val forgotCenterY = promptCenterY(portraitPromptForgotCenterY())
  1292	        val createBaselineY = createCenterY - (secondaryMetrics.ascent + secondaryMetrics.descent) / 2f
  1293	        val signInBaselineY = signInCenterY - (primaryMetrics.ascent + primaryMetrics.descent) / 2f
  1294	        val forgotBaselineY = forgotCenterY - (secondaryMetrics.ascent + secondaryMetrics.descent) / 2f
  1295	        val portraitCenterX = FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH / 2f
  1296	        if (
  1297	            activeMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount &&
  1298	            outgoingMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount
  1299	        ) {
  1300	            accountPromptPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  1301	            canvas.drawText(
  1302	                ACCOUNT_PROMPT_CREATE_TEXT,
  1303	                stageLeft + lerpFloat(accountPromptCreateCenterX(), portraitCenterX, portraitAmount) * stageScale,
  1304	                createBaselineY,
  1305	                accountPromptPaint
  1306	            )
  1307	        }
  1308	        if (
  1309	            activeMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.SignIn &&
  1310	            outgoingMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.SignIn
  1311	        ) {
  1312	            formTitlePaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  1313	            canvas.drawText(
  1314	                ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_TEXT,
  1315	                stageLeft + lerpFloat(ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_CENTER_X, portraitCenterX, portraitAmount) * stageScale,
  1316	                signInBaselineY,
  1317	                formTitlePaint
  1318	            )
  1319	        }
  1320	        if (
  1321	            activeMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword &&
  1322	            outgoingMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword
  1323	        ) {
  1324	            accountPromptPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  1325	            canvas.drawText(
  1326	                ACCOUNT_PROMPT_FORGOT_TEXT,
  1327	                stageLeft + lerpFloat(accountPromptForgotCenterX(), portraitCenterX, portraitAmount) * stageScale,
  1328	                forgotBaselineY,
  1329	                accountPromptPaint
  1330	            )
  1331	        }
  1332	        accountPromptPaint.alpha = 255
  1333	        accountPromptPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  1334	        formTitlePaint.alpha = 255
  1335	    }

  1337	    private fun drawMockSignInForm(
  1338	        canvas: Canvas,
  1339	        stageLeft: Float,
  1340	        stageTop: Float,
  1341	        stageScale: Float,
  1342	        progress: Float,
  1343	        formFocusMotion: Float,
  1344	        updateLabelAnimation: Boolean = true
  1345	    ) {
  1346	        if (updateLabelAnimation) {
  1347	            updateMockFormLabelAnimation()
  1348	        }
  1349	
  1350	        val alpha = progress.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  1351	        if (activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount) {
  1352	            drawMockCreateAccountForm(canvas, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha, formFocusMotion)
  1353	            return
  1354	        }
  1355	        if (activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword) {
  1356	            drawMockForgotPasswordForm(canvas, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha, formFocusMotion)
  1357	            return
  1358	        }
  1359	
  1360	        val yOffset = MOCK_FORM_ENTRY_Y * (1f - alpha)
  1361	        val fieldFillAlpha = (alpha * 26f).roundToInt()
  1362	        val fieldStrokeAlpha = (alpha * 185f).roundToInt()
  1363	        val fieldX = mockFormX()
  1364	        val usernameY = MOCK_FORM_Y + yOffset
  1365	        val passwordY = usernameY + MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT + MOCK_FORM_FIELD_GAP
  1366	
  1367	        formFieldFillPaint.color = Color.argb(fieldFillAlpha, 255, 255, 255)
  1368	        formFieldStrokePaint.color = Color.argb(fieldStrokeAlpha, 255, 255, 255)
  1369	        formFieldStrokePaint.strokeWidth = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_STROKE_WIDTH * stageScale
  1370	        formCaretPaint.strokeWidth = 1.45f * stageScale
  1371	
  1372	        val focusShiftY = mockFormFocusShiftY(formFocusMotion)
  1373	        val rememberMeY = passwordY + MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT + MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_TOP_GAP + focusShiftY
  1374	        drawMockSignInTitle(canvas, usernameY + focusShiftY, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
  1375	        drawMockSignInField(canvas, MockSignInField.Username, fieldX, usernameY + focusShiftY, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
  1376	        drawMockSignInField(canvas, MockSignInField.Password, fieldX, passwordY + focusShiftY, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
  1377	        drawRememberMeCheckbox(
  1378	            canvas,
  1379	            fieldX,
  1380	            rememberMeY,
  1381	            stageLeft,
  1382	            stageTop,
  1383	            stageScale,
  1384	            alpha
  1385	        )
  1386	        drawMockInputDimOverlayAndActiveControl(
  1387	            canvas,
  1388	            stageLeft,
  1389	            stageTop,
  1390	            stageScale,
  1391	            alpha,
  1392	            yOffset,
  1393	            focusShiftY
  1394	        )
  1395	
  1396	        formLabelPaint.alpha = 255
  1397	        formInputPaint.alpha = 255
  1398	        formTitlePaint.alpha = 255
  1399	        formCaretPaint.alpha = 255
  1400	    }

  1582	    private fun drawMockInputDimOverlayAndActiveControl(
  1583	        canvas: Canvas,
  1584	        stageLeft: Float,
  1585	        stageTop: Float,
  1586	        stageScale: Float,
  1587	        alpha: Float,
  1588	        yOffset: Float,
  1589	        focusShiftY: Float
  1590	    ) {
  1591	        val dimProgress = mockInputDimProgress.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  1592	        if (dimProgress <= 0.01f) return
  1593	
  1594	        inputDimPaint.alpha = (dimProgress * MOCK_INPUT_DIM_ALPHA * 255f).roundToInt()
  1595	        canvas.drawRect(0f, 0f, width.toFloat(), height.toFloat(), inputDimPaint)
  1596	        inputDimPaint.alpha = 255
  1597

  1599	        formFieldStrokePaint.color = Color.argb((alpha * 185f).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255)
  1600	        formFieldStrokePaint.strokeWidth = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_STROKE_WIDTH * stageScale
  1601	        formCaretPaint.strokeWidth = 1.45f * stageScale
  1602	
  1603	        val activeDropdown = expandedMockDropdown ?: mockInputDimActiveDropdown
  1604	        if (activeDropdown != null && activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount) {
  1605	            drawMockDropdownControl(
  1606	                canvas,
  1607	                activeDropdown,
  1608	                mockDropdownX(activeDropdown),
  1609	                mockDropdownY(activeDropdown, yOffset, focusShiftY),
  1610	                stageLeft,
  1611	                stageTop,
  1612	                stageScale,
  1613	                alpha,
  1614	                mockDropdownWidth(activeDropdown)
  1615	            )
  1616	            if (expandedMockDropdown == activeDropdown) {
  1617	                drawMockDropdownOptions(canvas, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
  1618	            }
  1619	            return
  1620	        }
  1621	
  1622	        val activeField = focusedMockField ?: mockInputDimActiveField ?: return
  1623	        drawMockSignInField(
  1624	            canvas,
  1625	            activeField,
  1626	            mockActiveFieldX(activeField),
  1627	            mockActiveFieldY(activeField, yOffset, focusShiftY),
  1628	            stageLeft,
  1629	            stageTop,
  1630	            stageScale,
  1631	            alpha,
  1632	            mockActiveFieldWidth(activeField)
  1633	        )
  1634	    }

  1636	    private fun drawMockSignInFormForFlow(
  1637	        canvas: Canvas,
  1638	        flow: MockAccountFlow,
  1639	        stageLeft: Float,
  1640	        stageTop: Float,
  1641	        stageScale: Float,
  1642	        progress: Float,
  1643	        formFocusMotion: Float,
  1644	        updateLabelAnimation: Boolean
  1645	    ) {
  1646	        val currentFlow = activeMockFlow
  1647	        activeMockFlow = flow
  1648	        try {
  1649	            drawMockSignInForm(
  1650	                canvas,
  1651	                stageLeft,
  1652	                stageTop,
  1653	                stageScale,
  1654	                progress,
  1655	                formFocusMotion,
  1656	                updateLabelAnimation
  1657	            )
  1658	        } finally {
  1659	            activeMockFlow = currentFlow
  1660	        }
  1661	    }
  1662	
  1663	    private fun drawMockSignInTitle(
  1664	        canvas: Canvas,
  1665	        fieldTopY: Float,
  1666	        stageLeft: Float,
  1667	        stageTop: Float,
  1668	        stageScale: Float,
  1669	        alpha: Float
  1670	    ) {
  1671	        val motion = alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  1672	        val sourceTextSize = signInAccountPromptTextSize() * stageScale
  1673	        val targetTextSize = MOCK_FORM_TITLE_TEXT_SIZE * stageScale
  1674	        val sourceCenterX = stageLeft + ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_CENTER_X * stageScale
  1675	        val targetCenterX = stageLeft + ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_CENTER_X * stageScale
  1676	        val sourceCenterY = stageTop +
  1677	            (ACCOUNT_PROMPT_CENTER_Y + signInStackShiftY() + signInAccountPromptExtraShiftY()) * stageScale
  1678	        formTitlePaint.textSize = sourceTextSize
  1679	        val sourceMetrics = formTitlePaint.fontMetrics
  1680	        val sourceBaselineY = sourceCenterY - (sourceMetrics.ascent + sourceMetrics.descent) / 2f
  1681	        val targetBaselineY = stageTop + (fieldTopY - MOCK_FORM_TITLE_BASELINE_GAP) * stageScale
  1682

  1687	            ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_TEXT,
  1688	            lerpFloat(sourceCenterX, targetCenterX, motion),
  1689	            lerpFloat(sourceBaselineY, targetBaselineY, motion),
  1690	            formTitlePaint
  1691	        )
  1692	    }

  1756	    private fun drawMockForgotPasswordButton(
  1757	        canvas: Canvas,
  1758	        x: Float,
  1759	        y: Float,
  1760	        stageLeft: Float,
  1761	        stageTop: Float,
  1762	        stageScale: Float,
  1763	        alpha: Float,
  1764	        buttonWidth: Float = MOCK_FORM_WIDTH
  1765	    ) {
  1766	        tempRect.set(
  1767	            stageLeft + x * stageScale,
  1768	            stageTop + y * stageScale,
  1769	            stageLeft + (x + buttonWidth) * stageScale,
  1770	            stageTop + (y + MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BUTTON_HEIGHT) * stageScale
  1771	        )
  1772	        val radius = MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BUTTON_RADIUS * stageScale
  1773	        val submissionState = forgotPasswordSubmissionState
  1774	        val enabled = mockForgotPasswordCanSubmit()
  1775	        val pressed = pressedForgotPasswordSubmit && enabled
  1776	        val fillAlpha = when {
  1777	            submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Sent -> 86f
  1778	            submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Loading -> 72f
  1779	            pressed -> 92f
  1780	            enabled -> 68f
  1781	            else -> 18f
  1782	        }
  1783	        val strokeAlpha = when {
  1784	            submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Sent -> 245f
  1785	            submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Loading -> 228f
  1786	            pressed -> 238f
  1787	            enabled -> 210f
  1788	            else -> 118f
  1789	        }
  1790	        formFieldFillPaint.shader = null
  1791	        formFieldFillPaint.color = Color.argb((alpha * fillAlpha).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255)
  1792	        formFieldStrokePaint.color = Color.argb((alpha * strokeAlpha).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255)
  1793	        formFieldStrokePaint.strokeWidth = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_STROKE_WIDTH * stageScale
  1794	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldFillPaint)
  1795	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldStrokePaint)
  1796	        if (submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Loading) {
  1797	            drawMockForgotPasswordButtonShimmer(canvas, tempRect, radius, alpha)
  1798	        }
  1799	
  1800	        formTitlePaint.textSize = MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_TEXT_SIZE * stageScale
  1801	        formTitlePaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  1802	        val metrics = formTitlePaint.fontMetrics
  1803	        val baselineY = tempRect.centerY() - (metrics.ascent + metrics.descent) / 2f
  1804	        val maxTextWidth = buttonWidth * stageScale - MOCK_FORM_LABEL_INSET_X * stageScale * 2f
  1805	        val showCheck = submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Sent
  1806	        val checkSize = if (showCheck) MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SENT_CHECK_SIZE * stageScale else 0f
  1807	        val checkGap = if (showCheck) MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SENT_CHECK_GAP * stageScale else 0f
  1808	        val textMaxWidth = (maxTextWidth - checkSize - checkGap).coerceAtLeast(maxTextWidth * 0.5f)
  1809	        val buttonText = trailingFittingText(
  1810	            mockForgotPasswordButtonText(),
  1811	            if (showCheck) textMaxWidth else maxTextWidth,
  1812	            formTitlePaint
  1813	        )
  1814	        val textAlpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * when {
  1815	            submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Loading -> 226f
  1816	            submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Sent -> 255f
  1817	            enabled -> 255f
  1818	            else -> 138f
  1819	        }).roundToInt()
  1820	        formTitlePaint.alpha = textAlpha
  1821	        val textX = if (showCheck) {
  1822	            val textWidth = formTitlePaint.measureText(buttonText)
  1823	            val groupWidth = checkSize + checkGap + textWidth
  1824	            val groupLeft = tempRect.centerX() - groupWidth / 2f
  1825	            val checkLeft = groupLeft
  1826	            val checkCenterY = tempRect.centerY()
  1827	            backChevronPaint.alpha = textAlpha
  1828	            backChevronPaint.strokeWidth = 2.1f * stageScale
  1829	            canvas.drawLine(
  1830	                checkLeft + checkSize * 0.18f,
  1831	                checkCenterY + checkSize * 0.03f,
  1832	                checkLeft + checkSize * 0.42f,
  1833	                checkCenterY + checkSize * 0.26f,
  1834	                backChevronPaint
  1835	            )
  1836	            canvas.drawLine(
  1837	                checkLeft + checkSize * 0.42f,
  1838	                checkCenterY + checkSize * 0.26f,
  1839	                checkLeft + checkSize * 0.84f,
  1840	                checkCenterY - checkSize * 0.28f,
  1841	                backChevronPaint
  1842	            )
  1843	            groupLeft + checkSize + checkGap + textWidth / 2f
  1844	        } else {
  1845	            tempRect.centerX()
  1846	        }
  1847	        canvas.drawText(
  1848	            buttonText,
  1849	            textX,
  1850	            baselineY,
  1851	            formTitlePaint
  1852	        )
  1853	    }
  1854	
  1855	    private fun drawMockForgotPasswordButtonShimmer(
  1856	        canvas: Canvas,
  1857	        buttonRect: RectF,
  1858	        radius: Float,
  1859	        alpha: Float
  1860	    ) {
  1861	        val progress = mockForgotPasswordSubmissionProgress()
  1862	        val sweepWidth = buttonRect.width() * 0.44f
  1863	        val sweepCenterX = buttonRect.left + buttonRect.width() * (progress * 1.35f - 0.18f)
  1864	        val sweepLeft = sweepCenterX - sweepWidth / 2f
  1865	        val sweepRight = sweepCenterX + sweepWidth / 2f
  1866	        formFieldFillPaint.shader = LinearGradient(
  1867	            sweepLeft,
  1868	            buttonRect.top,
  1869	            sweepRight,
  1870	            buttonRect.top,
  1871	            intArrayOf(
  1872	                Color.argb(0, 255, 255, 255),
  1873	                Color.argb((alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 54f).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255),
  1874	                Color.argb(0, 255, 255, 255)
  1875	            ),
  1876	            floatArrayOf(0f, 0.5f, 1f),
  1877	            Shader.TileMode.CLAMP
  1878	        )
  1879	        canvas.save()
  1880	        tempPath.reset()
  1881	        tempPath.addRoundRect(buttonRect, radius, radius, Path.Direction.CW)
  1882	        canvas.clipPath(tempPath)
  1883	        canvas.drawRect(sweepLeft, buttonRect.top, sweepRight, buttonRect.bottom, formFieldFillPaint)
  1884	        canvas.restore()
  1885	        formFieldFillPaint.shader = null
  1886	    }

  1945	    private fun drawMockForgotRecoveryOption(
  1946	        canvas: Canvas,
  1947	        target: MockForgotRecoveryTarget,
  1948	        left: Float,
  1949	        top: Float,
  1950	        boxWidth: Float,
  1951	        stageScale: Float,
  1952	        alpha: Float
  1953	    ) {
  1954	        val right = left + boxWidth
  1955	        val bottom = top + MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SELECTOR_BOX_HEIGHT * stageScale
  1956	        tempRect.set(left, top, right, bottom)
  1957	        val selected = forgotRecoveryTarget == target
  1958	        val pressed = pressedForgotRecoveryTarget == target
  1959	        val fillAlpha = (alpha * when {
  1960	            pressed -> 82f
  1961	            selected -> 64f
  1962	            else -> 14f
  1963	        }).roundToInt()
  1964	        val strokeAlpha = (alpha * when {
  1965	            pressed -> 245f
  1966	            selected -> 235f
  1967	            else -> 142f
  1968	        }).roundToInt()
  1969	        val radius = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_RADIUS * stageScale
  1970	
  1971	        formFieldFillPaint.color = Color.argb(fillAlpha, 255, 255, 255)
  1972	        formFieldStrokePaint.color = Color.argb(strokeAlpha, 255, 255, 255)
  1973	        formFieldStrokePaint.strokeWidth = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_STROKE_WIDTH * stageScale
  1974	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldFillPaint)
  1975	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldStrokePaint)
  1976	
  1977	        formInputPaint.textSize = MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SELECTOR_OPTION_TEXT_SIZE * stageScale
  1978	        formInputPaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * if (selected || pressed) 255f else 178f).roundToInt()
  1979	        formInputPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  1980	        val metrics = formInputPaint.fontMetrics
  1981	        canvas.drawText(
  1982	            target.displayText,
  1983	            tempRect.centerX(),
  1984	            tempRect.centerY() - (metrics.ascent + metrics.descent) / 2f,
  1985	            formInputPaint
  1986	        )
  1987	    }

  1989	    private fun drawMockForgotPasswordBodyParagraph(
  1990	        canvas: Canvas,
  1991	        text: String,
  1992	        centerX: Float,
  1993	        topY: Float,
  1994	        maxWidth: Float,
  1995	        stageLeft: Float,
  1996	        stageTop: Float,
  1997	        stageScale: Float,
  1998	        alpha: Float
  1999	    ): Float {
  2000	        val paragraphs = text.split(Regex("\\n\\s*\\n"))
  2001	        val leadBottomY = drawMockFormParagraph(
  2002	            canvas,
  2003	            paragraphs.firstOrNull().orEmpty(),
  2004	            centerX,
  2005	            topY,
  2006	            maxWidth,
  2007	            stageLeft,
  2008	            stageTop,
  2009	            stageScale,
  2010	            alpha,
  2011	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_LEAD_TEXT_SIZE,
  2012	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_LEAD_LINE_HEIGHT
  2013	        )
  2014	        val middleText = paragraphs.getOrNull(1) ?: return leadBottomY
  2015	        val middleBottomY = drawMockFormParagraph(
  2016	            canvas,
  2017	            middleText,
  2018	            centerX,
  2019	            leadBottomY + MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_LINE_HEIGHT,
  2020	            maxWidth,
  2021	            stageLeft,
  2022	            stageTop,
  2023	            stageScale,
  2024	            alpha,
  2025	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_MIDDLE_TEXT_SIZE,
  2026	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_MIDDLE_LINE_HEIGHT,
  2027	            Paint.Align.LEFT
  2028	        )
  2029	        val bottomText = paragraphs.drop(2).joinToString("\n\n")
  2030	        if (bottomText.isEmpty()) return middleBottomY
  2031	        return drawMockFormParagraph(
  2032	            canvas,
  2033	            bottomText,
  2034	            centerX,
  2035	            middleBottomY + MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_LINE_HEIGHT,
  2036	            maxWidth,
  2037	            stageLeft,
  2038	            stageTop,
  2039	            stageScale,
  2040	            alpha,
  2041	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_MIDDLE_TEXT_SIZE,
  2042	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_MIDDLE_LINE_HEIGHT,
  2043	            Paint.Align.LEFT
  2044	        )
  2045	    }
  2046	
  2047	    private fun drawMockFormParagraph(
  2048	        canvas: Canvas,
  2049	        text: String,
  2050	        centerX: Float,
  2051	        topY: Float,
  2052	        maxWidth: Float,
  2053	        stageLeft: Float,
  2054	        stageTop: Float,
  2055	        stageScale: Float,
  2056	        alpha: Float,
  2057	        textSize: Float,
  2058	        lineHeight: Float,
  2059	        textAlign: Paint.Align = Paint.Align.CENTER
  2060	    ): Float {
  2061	        formInputPaint.textSize = textSize * stageScale
  2062	        formInputPaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 218f).roundToInt()
  2063	        formInputPaint.textAlign = textAlign
  2064	
  2065	        val lines = wrappedTextLines(text, maxWidth * stageScale, formInputPaint)
  2066	        val metrics = formInputPaint.fontMetrics
  2067	        val x = when (textAlign) {
  2068	            Paint.Align.LEFT -> stageLeft + (centerX - maxWidth / 2f) * stageScale
  2069	            Paint.Align.RIGHT -> stageLeft + (centerX + maxWidth / 2f) * stageScale
  2070	            Paint.Align.CENTER -> stageLeft + centerX * stageScale
  2071	        }
  2072	        var baselineY = stageTop + topY * stageScale - metrics.ascent
  2073	        lines.forEach { line ->
  2074	            canvas.drawText(line, x, baselineY, formInputPaint)
  2075	            baselineY += lineHeight * stageScale
  2076	        }
  2077	        return topY + lines.size * lineHeight
  2078	    }

  2080	    private fun drawMockCreateAccountAvatarText(
  2081	        canvas: Canvas,
  2082	        columnX: Float,
  2083	        firstFieldY: Float,
  2084	        stageLeft: Float,
  2085	        stageTop: Float,
  2086	        stageScale: Float,
  2087	        alpha: Float
  2088	    ) {
  2089	        val avatarScale = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_STACK_SCALE
  2090	        val scaledAvatarSize = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_SIZE * avatarScale
  2091	        val labelBaselineFromTop = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_LABEL_BASELINE_FROM_TOP * avatarScale
  2092	        val avatarTopGap = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_TOP_GAP * avatarScale
  2093	        val centerX = stageLeft + (columnX + MOCK_FORM_WIDTH / 2f) * stageScale
  2094	        val baselineY = stageTop + (firstFieldY + labelBaselineFromTop) * stageScale
  2095	        formLabelPaint.textSize = MOCK_FORM_LABEL_TEXT_SIZE * avatarScale * stageScale
  2096	        formLabelPaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
  2097	        formLabelPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  2098	        canvas.drawText(
  2099	            MOCK_FORM_AVATAR_TEXT,
  2100	            centerX,
  2101	            baselineY,
  2102	            formLabelPaint
  2103	        )
  2104	
  2105	        val avatarLeftX = columnX + (MOCK_FORM_WIDTH - scaledAvatarSize) / 2f
  2106	        val avatarTopY = firstFieldY + labelBaselineFromTop + avatarTopGap
  2107	        val avatarBounds = Bounds(
  2108	            avatarLeftX,
  2109	            avatarTopY,
  2110	            scaledAvatarSize,
  2111	            scaledAvatarSize
  2112	        )
  2113	        val carouselProgress = mockCreateAvatarCarouselProgress()
  2114	        val slideDirection = mockCreateAvatarCarouselDirection.stageDirection
  2115	        val slideDistance = mockCreateAvatarSlideDistance(scaledAvatarSize)
  2116	        val dragDirection = mockCreateAvatarDragDirection()
  2117	        val dragProgress = mockCreateAvatarDragProgress(slideDistance, stageScale)
  2118	
  2119	        drawMockCreateAvatarBubbleShell(canvas, avatarBounds, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
  2120	
  2121	        canvas.save()
  2122	        clipMockCreateAvatarBubbleInterior(canvas, avatarBounds, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale)
  2123	        if (
  2124	            activeMockCreateAvatarDrag &&
  2125	            mockCreateAvatarDragMoved &&
  2126	            dragDirection != null &&
  2127	            !isMockCreateAvatarCarouselAnimating()
  2128	        ) {
  2129	            val dragSlideDirection = dragDirection.stageDirection
  2130	            drawMockCreateAvatarBitmap(
  2131	                canvas,
  2132	                mockCreateAvatarIndex,
  2133	                avatarBounds,
  2134	                -dragSlideDirection * dragProgress * slideDistance,
  2135	                stageLeft,
  2136	                stageTop,
  2137	                stageScale,
  2138	                alpha
  2139	            )
  2140	            drawMockCreateAvatarBitmap(
  2141	                canvas,
  2142	                mockCreateAvatarIndexForDirection(dragDirection),
  2143	                avatarBounds,
  2144	                dragSlideDirection * (1f - dragProgress) * slideDistance,
  2145	                stageLeft,
  2146	                stageTop,
  2147	                stageScale,
  2148	                alpha
  2149	            )
  2150	        } else if (isMockCreateAvatarCarouselAnimating()) {
  2151	            drawMockCreateAvatarBitmap(
  2152	                canvas,
  2153	                mockCreateAvatarCarouselFromIndex,
  2154	                avatarBounds,
  2155	                -slideDirection * carouselProgress * slideDistance,
  2156	                stageLeft,
  2157	                stageTop,
  2158	                stageScale,
  2159	                alpha
  2160	            )
  2161	            drawMockCreateAvatarBitmap(
  2162	                canvas,
  2163	                mockCreateAvatarCarouselToIndex,
  2164	                avatarBounds,
  2165	                slideDirection * (1f - carouselProgress) * slideDistance,
  2166	                stageLeft,
  2167	                stageTop,
  2168	                stageScale,
  2169	                alpha
  2170	            )
  2171	        } else {
  2172	            drawMockCreateAvatarBitmap(
  2173	                canvas,
  2174	                mockCreateAvatarIndex,
  2175	                avatarBounds,
  2176	                0f,
  2177	                stageLeft,
  2178	                stageTop,
  2179	                stageScale,
  2180	                alpha
  2181	            )
  2182	        }
  2183	        canvas.restore()
  2184	        drawMockCreateAvatarChevron(
  2185	            canvas,
  2186	            MockAvatarCarouselDirection.Previous,
  2187	            avatarBounds,
  2188	            stageLeft,
  2189	            stageTop,
  2190	            stageScale,
  2191	            alpha
  2192	        )
  2193	        drawMockCreateAvatarChevron(
  2194	            canvas,
  2195	            MockAvatarCarouselDirection.Next,
  2196	            avatarBounds,
  2197	            stageLeft,
  2198	            stageTop,
  2199	            stageScale,
  2200	            alpha
  2201	        )
  2202	    }
  2203	
  2204	    private fun drawMockCreateAvatarBitmap(
  2205	        canvas: Canvas,
  2206	        avatarIndex: Int,
  2207	        bounds: Bounds,
  2208	        offsetX: Float,
  2209	        stageLeft: Float,
  2210	        stageTop: Float,
  2211	        stageScale: Float,
  2212	        alpha: Float
  2213	    ) {
  2214	        drawFigmaBitmap(

  2265	    private fun drawMockCreateAvatarChevron(
  2266	        canvas: Canvas,
  2267	        direction: MockAvatarCarouselDirection,
  2268	        avatarBounds: Bounds,
  2269	        stageLeft: Float,
  2270	        stageTop: Float,
  2271	        stageScale: Float,
  2272	        alpha: Float
  2273	    ) {
  2274	        val centerX = stageLeft + mockCreateAvatarChevronCenterX(direction, avatarBounds) * stageScale
  2275	        val centerY = stageTop + mockCreateAvatarChevronCenterY(avatarBounds) * stageScale
  2276	        val halfWidth = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CHEVRON_WIDTH * stageScale / 2f
  2277	        val halfHeight = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CHEVRON_HEIGHT * stageScale / 2f
  2278	        val pressedAlpha = if (pressedMockCreateAvatarNav == direction) 0.62f else 0.9f
  2279	        backChevronPaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * pressedAlpha * 255f).roundToInt()
  2280	        backChevronPaint.strokeWidth = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CHEVRON_STROKE_WIDTH * stageScale
  2281	
  2282	        if (direction == MockAvatarCarouselDirection.Previous) {
  2283	            canvas.drawLine(centerX + halfWidth, centerY - halfHeight, centerX - halfWidth, centerY, backChevronPaint)
  2284	            canvas.drawLine(centerX - halfWidth, centerY, centerX + halfWidth, centerY + halfHeight, backChevronPaint)
  2285	        } else {
  2286	            canvas.drawLine(centerX - halfWidth, centerY - halfHeight, centerX + halfWidth, centerY, backChevronPaint)
  2287	            canvas.drawLine(centerX + halfWidth, centerY, centerX - halfWidth, centerY + halfHeight, backChevronPaint)
  2288	        }
  2289	        backChevronPaint.alpha = 255
  2290	    }
