  2292	    private fun drawMockDropdownControl(
  2293	        canvas: Canvas,
  2294	        dropdown: MockDropdown,
  2295	        x: Float,
  2296	        y: Float,
  2297	        stageLeft: Float,
  2298	        stageTop: Float,
  2299	        stageScale: Float,
  2300	        alpha: Float,
  2301	        dropdownWidth: Float = MOCK_FORM_WIDTH
  2302	    ) {
  2303	        tempRect.set(
  2304	            stageLeft + x * stageScale,
  2305	            stageTop + y * stageScale,
  2306	            stageLeft + (x + dropdownWidth) * stageScale,
  2307	            stageTop + (y + MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT) * stageScale
  2308	        )
  2309	        val radius = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_RADIUS * stageScale
  2310	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldFillPaint)
  2311	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldStrokePaint)
  2312	
  2313	        val textAlpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
  2314	        formInputPaint.textSize = mockDropdownTextSize(dropdown) * stageScale
  2315	        formInputPaint.alpha = textAlpha
  2316	        formInputPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.LEFT
  2317	        val textInset = mockDropdownTextInsetX(dropdown) * stageScale
  2318	        val chevronInset = mockDropdownChevronInsetX(dropdown) * stageScale
  2319	        val displayText = mockDropdownDisplayText(dropdown)
  2320	        val membershipIcon = membershipTierArtworkIconForOption(displayText)
  2321	            ?.takeIf { dropdown == MockDropdown.SubscriptionTier }
  2322	        val iconLaneWidth = if (membershipIcon != null) {
  2323	            (MOCK_MEMBERSHIP_TIER_CONTROL_ICON_SIZE + MOCK_MEMBERSHIP_TIER_ICON_TEXT_GAP) * stageScale
  2324	        } else {
  2325	            0f
  2326	        }
  2327	        val maxTextWidth = (dropdownWidth * stageScale - textInset - iconLaneWidth - chevronInset * 2f)
  2328	            .coerceAtLeast(0f)
  2329	        if (membershipIcon != null) {
  2330	            drawMembershipTierIcon(
  2331	                canvas = canvas,
  2332	                bitmap = membershipIcon,
  2333	                left = tempRect.left + textInset,
  2334	                centerY = tempRect.centerY(),
  2335	                iconSize = MOCK_MEMBERSHIP_TIER_CONTROL_ICON_SIZE * stageScale,
  2336	                alpha = textAlpha
  2337	            )
  2338	        }
  2339	        canvas.drawText(
  2340	            trailingFittingText(displayText, maxTextWidth, formInputPaint),
  2341	            tempRect.left + textInset + iconLaneWidth,
  2342	            tempRect.centerY() - (formInputPaint.fontMetrics.ascent + formInputPaint.fontMetrics.descent) / 2f,
  2343	            formInputPaint
  2344	        )
  2345	
  2346	        val centerX = tempRect.right - chevronInset
  2347	        val centerY = tempRect.centerY() + 2f * stageScale
  2348	        val halfSize = mockDropdownChevronSize(dropdown) * stageScale / 2f
  2349	        backChevronPaint.alpha = textAlpha
  2350	        backChevronPaint.strokeWidth = 1.65f * stageScale
  2351	        canvas.drawLine(centerX - halfSize, centerY - halfSize / 2f, centerX, centerY + halfSize / 2f, backChevronPaint)
  2352	        canvas.drawLine(centerX, centerY + halfSize / 2f, centerX + halfSize, centerY - halfSize / 2f, backChevronPaint)
  2353	        backChevronPaint.alpha = 255
  2354	    }

  2356	    private fun drawMockDropdownOptions(
  2357	        canvas: Canvas,
  2358	        stageLeft: Float,
  2359	        stageTop: Float,
  2360	        stageScale: Float,
  2361	        alpha: Float
  2362	    ) {
  2363	        val dropdown = expandedMockDropdown ?: return
  2364	        if (activeMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount) return
  2365	
  2366	        val x = mockDropdownX(dropdown)
  2367	        val y = mockDropdownOptionsY(
  2368	            dropdown,
  2369	            MOCK_FORM_ENTRY_Y * (1f - alpha),
  2370	            mockFormFocusShiftY(FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFormFocusProgress))
  2371	        )
  2372	        val left = stageLeft + x * stageScale
  2373	        val top = stageTop + y * stageScale
  2374	        val right = stageLeft + (x + mockDropdownWidth(dropdown)) * stageScale
  2375	        val optionHeight = mockDropdownOptionHeight(dropdown) * stageScale
  2376	        val optionAlpha = 255
  2377	        val optionTextInset = mockDropdownOptionTextInsetX(dropdown) * stageScale
  2378	        val scrollbarReservedWidth = if (mockDropdownCanScroll(dropdown)) {
  2379	            (MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_WIDTH + MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_INSET_X * 2f) * stageScale
  2380	        } else {
  2381	            0f
  2382	        }
  2383	
  2384	        val visibleOptions = mockDropdownVisibleOptions(dropdown)
  2385	        visibleOptions.forEachIndexed { index, option ->
  2386	            val optionTop = top + index * optionHeight
  2387	            val optionBottom = optionTop + optionHeight
  2388	            val fillColor = if (option == mockDropdownSelectedValue(dropdown)) {
  2389	                Color.argb(optionAlpha, 76, 12, 94)
  2390	            } else {
  2391	                Color.argb(optionAlpha, 14, 2, 18)
  2392	            }
  2393	            tempRect.set(left, optionTop, right, optionBottom)
  2394	            formFieldFillPaint.color = fillColor
  2395	            formFieldStrokePaint.color = Color.argb(optionAlpha, 255, 255, 255)
  2396	            formFieldStrokePaint.strokeWidth = 1.2f * stageScale
  2397	            canvas.drawRect(tempRect, formFieldFillPaint)
  2398	            canvas.drawRect(tempRect, formFieldStrokePaint)
  2399	
  2400	            formInputPaint.textSize = mockDropdownOptionTextSize(dropdown) * stageScale
  2401	            formInputPaint.alpha = optionAlpha
  2402	            formInputPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.LEFT
  2403	            val metrics = formInputPaint.fontMetrics
  2404	            val membershipIcon = membershipTierArtworkIconForOption(option)
  2405	                ?.takeIf { dropdown == MockDropdown.SubscriptionTier }
  2406	            val iconLaneWidth = if (membershipIcon != null) {
  2407	                (MOCK_MEMBERSHIP_TIER_OPTION_ICON_SIZE + MOCK_MEMBERSHIP_TIER_ICON_TEXT_GAP) * stageScale
  2408	            } else {
  2409	                0f
  2410	            }
  2411	            if (membershipIcon != null) {
  2412	                drawMembershipTierIcon(
  2413	                    canvas = canvas,
  2414	                    bitmap = membershipIcon,
  2415	                    left = left + optionTextInset,
  2416	                    centerY = (optionTop + optionBottom) / 2f,
  2417	                    iconSize = MOCK_MEMBERSHIP_TIER_OPTION_ICON_SIZE * stageScale,
  2418	                    alpha = optionAlpha
  2419	                )
  2420	            }
  2421	            val maxTextWidth = (right - left - optionTextInset - iconLaneWidth - scrollbarReservedWidth)
  2422	                .coerceAtLeast(0f)
  2423	            canvas.drawText(
  2424	                trailingFittingText(option, maxTextWidth, formInputPaint),
  2425	                left + optionTextInset + iconLaneWidth,
  2426	                (optionTop + optionBottom) / 2f - (metrics.ascent + metrics.descent) / 2f,
  2427	                formInputPaint
  2428	            )
  2429	        }
  2430	
  2431	        if (mockDropdownCanScroll(dropdown)) {
  2432	            drawMockDropdownScrollbar(
  2433	                canvas,
  2434	                dropdown,
  2435	                top,
  2436	                right,
  2437	                optionHeight * visibleOptions.size,
  2438	                stageScale,
  2439	                optionAlpha
  2440	            )
  2441	        }
  2442	    }

  2444	    private fun drawMembershipTierIcon(
  2445	        canvas: Canvas,
  2446	        bitmap: Bitmap,
  2447	        left: Float,
  2448	        centerY: Float,
  2449	        iconSize: Float,
  2450	        alpha: Int
  2451	    ) {
  2452	        val bitmapAspect = bitmap.width.toFloat() / bitmap.height.toFloat()
  2453	        val width: Float
  2454	        val height: Float
  2455	        if (bitmapAspect >= 1f) {
  2456	            width = iconSize
  2457	            height = iconSize / bitmapAspect
  2458	        } else {
  2459	            width = iconSize * bitmapAspect
  2460	            height = iconSize
  2461	        }
  2462	        val top = centerY - height / 2f
  2463	        tempIconRect.set(left, top, left + width, top + height)
  2464	        imagePaint.alpha = alpha.coerceIn(0, 255)
  2465	        canvas.drawBitmap(bitmap, null, tempIconRect, imagePaint)
  2466	        imagePaint.alpha = 255
  2467	    }

  2469	    private fun drawMockDropdownScrollbar(
  2470	        canvas: Canvas,
  2471	        dropdown: MockDropdown,
  2472	        top: Float,
  2473	        right: Float,
  2474	        dropdownHeight: Float,
  2475	        stageScale: Float,
  2476	        alpha: Int
  2477	    ) {
  2478	        val options = mockDropdownOptions(dropdown)
  2479	        val visibleCount = mockDropdownVisibleOptionCount(dropdown)
  2480	        val maxOffset = mockDropdownMaxScrollOffset(dropdown)
  2481	        if (options.isEmpty() || maxOffset <= 0) return
  2482	
  2483	        val trackRight = right - MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_INSET_X * stageScale
  2484	        val trackLeft = trackRight - MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_WIDTH * stageScale
  2485	        val trackTop = top + MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_INSET_Y * stageScale
  2486	        val trackBottom = top + dropdownHeight - MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_INSET_Y * stageScale
  2487	        val trackHeight = (trackBottom - trackTop).coerceAtLeast(0f)
  2488	        val thumbHeight = maxOf(14f * stageScale, trackHeight * visibleCount / options.size)
  2489	            .coerceAtMost(trackHeight)
  2490	        val thumbProgress = mockDropdownScrollOffset(dropdown) / maxOffset.toFloat()
  2491	        val thumbTop = trackTop + (trackHeight - thumbHeight) * thumbProgress
  2492	        val radius = MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_WIDTH * stageScale / 2f
  2493	
  2494	        tempRect.set(trackLeft, trackTop, trackRight, trackBottom)
  2495	        formFieldFillPaint.color = Color.argb((alpha * 0.28f).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255)
  2496	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldFillPaint)
  2497	
  2498	        tempRect.set(trackLeft, thumbTop, trackRight, thumbTop + thumbHeight)
  2499	        formFieldFillPaint.color = Color.argb((alpha * 0.78f).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255)
  2500	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldFillPaint)
  2501	    }

  2503	    private fun drawMockSignInField(
  2504	        canvas: Canvas,
  2505	        field: MockSignInField,
  2506	        x: Float,
  2507	        y: Float,
  2508	        stageLeft: Float,
  2509	        stageTop: Float,
  2510	        stageScale: Float,
  2511	        alpha: Float,
  2512	        fieldWidth: Float = MOCK_FORM_WIDTH
  2513	    ) {
  2514	        tempRect.set(
  2515	            stageLeft + x * stageScale,
  2516	            stageTop + y * stageScale,
  2517	            stageLeft + (x + fieldWidth) * stageScale,
  2518	            stageTop + (y + MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT) * stageScale
  2519	        )
  2520	        val radius = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_RADIUS * stageScale
  2521	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldFillPaint)
  2522	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldStrokePaint)
  2523	
  2524	        val label = mockSignInFieldLabel(field)
  2525	        val labelProgress = mockSignInFieldLabelProgress(field)
  2526	        val textAlpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
  2527	        formLabelPaint.textSize = lerpFloat(
  2528	            MOCK_FORM_LABEL_TEXT_SIZE,
  2529	            MOCK_FORM_LABEL_TEXT_SIZE * MOCK_FORM_FLOATING_LABEL_SCALE,
  2530	            labelProgress
  2531	        ) * stageScale
  2532	        formLabelPaint.alpha = textAlpha
  2533	        formLabelPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.LEFT
  2534	        val metrics = formLabelPaint.fontMetrics
  2535	        val placeholderBaselineY = tempRect.centerY() - (metrics.ascent + metrics.descent) / 2f
  2536	        val floatingBaselineY = tempRect.top + MOCK_FORM_LABEL_FLOAT_TOP_INSET_Y * stageScale - metrics.ascent
  2537	        val labelX = lerpFloat(
  2538	            tempRect.left + MOCK_FORM_LABEL_INSET_X * stageScale,
  2539	            tempRect.left + MOCK_FORM_LABEL_FLOAT_INSET_X * stageScale,
  2540	            labelProgress
  2541	        )
  2542	        val labelBaselineY = lerpFloat(placeholderBaselineY, floatingBaselineY, labelProgress)
  2543	        canvas.drawText(
  2544	            label,
  2545	            labelX,
  2546	            labelBaselineY,
  2547	            formLabelPaint
  2548	        )
  2549	
  2550	        val fieldText = mockSignInFieldDisplayText(field)
  2551	        val shouldDrawInput = fieldText.isNotEmpty() || focusedMockField == field
  2552	        if (shouldDrawInput) {
  2553	            val inputAlpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * labelProgress * 255f).roundToInt()
  2554	            formInputPaint.textSize = MOCK_FORM_LABEL_TEXT_SIZE * stageScale
  2555	            formInputPaint.alpha = inputAlpha
  2556	            formInputPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.LEFT
  2557	            formCaretPaint.alpha = inputAlpha
  2558	
  2559	            val inputX = tempRect.left + MOCK_FORM_LABEL_INSET_X * stageScale
  2560	            val inputBaselineY = tempRect.top + MOCK_FORM_INPUT_BASELINE_FROM_TOP * stageScale
  2561	            val maxInputWidth = fieldWidth * stageScale - MOCK_FORM_LABEL_INSET_X * stageScale * 2f
  2562	            val visibleText = trailingFittingText(fieldText, maxInputWidth, formInputPaint)
  2563	            canvas.drawText(visibleText, inputX, inputBaselineY, formInputPaint)
  2564	
  2565	            if (focusedMockField == field) {
  2566	                val caretX = inputX + formInputPaint.measureText(visibleText) + 2f * stageScale
  2567	                val inputMetrics = formInputPaint.fontMetrics
  2568	                canvas.drawLine(
  2569	                    caretX,
  2570	                    inputBaselineY + inputMetrics.ascent,
  2571	                    caretX,
  2572	                    inputBaselineY + inputMetrics.descent,
  2573	                    formCaretPaint
  2574	                )
  2575	            }
  2576	        }
  2577	    }

  2579	    private fun drawRememberMeCheckbox(
  2580	        canvas: Canvas,
  2581	        x: Float,
  2582	        y: Float,
  2583	        stageLeft: Float,
  2584	        stageTop: Float,
  2585	        stageScale: Float,
  2586	        alpha: Float
  2587	    ) {

  2589	        val boxLeft = stageLeft + x * stageScale

  2592	        val boxBottom = boxTop + MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_BOX_SIZE * stageScale
  2593	        val fillAlpha = (alpha * if (rememberMeChecked || pressedRememberMe) 46f else 18f).roundToInt()
  2594	        val strokeAlpha = (alpha * if (rememberMeChecked) 220f else 168f).roundToInt()
  2595

  2600	        val radius = MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_BOX_RADIUS * stageScale
  2601	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, rememberMeBoxFillPaint)
  2602	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, rememberMeBoxStrokePaint)

  2605	            rememberMeCheckPaint.alpha = paintAlpha
  2606	            rememberMeCheckPaint.strokeWidth = 2.45f * stageScale
  2607	            canvas.drawLine(
  2608	                boxLeft + 5.3f * stageScale,
  2609	                boxTop + 11.8f * stageScale,

  2611	                boxTop + 15.8f * stageScale,
  2612	                rememberMeCheckPaint

  2614	            canvas.drawLine(
  2615	                boxLeft + 9.1f * stageScale,
  2616	                boxTop + 15.8f * stageScale,
  2617	                boxLeft + 17.2f * stageScale,
  2618	                boxTop + 6.9f * stageScale,
  2619	                rememberMeCheckPaint
  2620	            )
  2621	            rememberMeCheckPaint.alpha = 255
  2622	        }
  2623	
  2624	        rememberMeTextPaint.textSize = MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_TEXT_SIZE * stageScale
  2625	        rememberMeTextPaint.alpha = paintAlpha
  2626	        rememberMeTextPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.LEFT
  2627	        val metrics = rememberMeTextPaint.fontMetrics
  2628	        val baselineY = (boxTop + boxBottom) / 2f - (metrics.ascent + metrics.descent) / 2f
  2629	        canvas.drawText(
  2630	            MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_TEXT,
  2631	            boxRight + MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_LABEL_GAP * stageScale,
  2632	            baselineY,
  2633	            rememberMeTextPaint
  2634	        )
  2635	        rememberMeTextPaint.alpha = 255
  2636	    }

  2638	    private fun drawBackChevron(
  2639	        canvas: Canvas,
  2640	        centerX: Float,
  2641	        topY: Float,
  2642	        stageLeft: Float,
  2643	        stageTop: Float,
  2644	        stageScale: Float,
  2645	        alpha: Float
  2646	    ) {
  2647	        val chevronAlpha = alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * if (pressedBackChevron) 0.68f else 0.92f
  2648	        val center = stageLeft + centerX * stageScale
  2649	        val top = stageTop + topY * stageScale
  2650	        val bottom = top + MOCK_BACK_CHEVRON_HEIGHT * stageScale
  2651	        val halfWidth = MOCK_BACK_CHEVRON_WIDTH * stageScale / 2f
  2652	
  2653	        backChevronPaint.alpha = (chevronAlpha * 255f).roundToInt()
  2654	        backChevronPaint.strokeWidth = MOCK_BACK_CHEVRON_STROKE_WIDTH * stageScale
  2655	        canvas.drawLine(center - halfWidth, top, center, bottom, backChevronPaint)
  2656	        canvas.drawLine(center, bottom, center + halfWidth, top, backChevronPaint)
  2657	        backChevronPaint.alpha = 255
  2658	    }

  2660	    private fun decode(resId: Int): Bitmap {
  2661	        return BitmapFactory.decodeResource(resources, resId, bitmapOptions)
  2662	    }
  2663	
  2664	    private fun createAvatarCharacterBitmap(
  2665	        source: Bitmap,
  2666	        includeDarkSaturatedPixels: Boolean = false
  2667	    ): Bitmap {
  2668	        val bitmapWidth = source.width
  2669	        val bitmapHeight = source.height
  2670	        val pixels = IntArray(bitmapWidth * bitmapHeight)
  2671	        source.getPixels(pixels, 0, bitmapWidth, 0, 0, bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight)
  2672	
  2673	        val mask = createAvatarCharacterMask(pixels, bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight, includeDarkSaturatedPixels)
  2674	        val outputPixels = IntArray(pixels.size) { index ->
  2675	            if (mask[index]) pixels[index] else Color.TRANSPARENT
  2676	        }
  2677	        return Bitmap.createBitmap(bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight, Bitmap.Config.ARGB_8888).apply {
  2678	            setPixels(outputPixels, 0, bitmapWidth, 0, 0, bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight)
  2679	        }
  2680	    }
  2681	
  2682	    private fun createAvatarBubbleShellBitmap(source: Bitmap): Bitmap {
  2683	        val bitmapWidth = source.width
  2684	        val bitmapHeight = source.height
  2685	        val pixels = IntArray(bitmapWidth * bitmapHeight)
  2686	        source.getPixels(pixels, 0, bitmapWidth, 0, 0, bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight)
  2687	
  2688	        val centerX = (bitmapWidth - 1f) / 2f
  2689	        val centerY = (bitmapHeight - 1f) / 2f
  2690	        val innerRadius = min(bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight) * (0.5f - MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_BUBBLE_INNER_INSET_RATIO)
  2691	        val innerRadiusSquared = innerRadius * innerRadius
  2692	
  2693	        val outputPixels = IntArray(pixels.size) { index ->
  2694	            val x = index % bitmapWidth
  2695	            val y = index / bitmapWidth
  2696	            val dx = x - centerX
  2697	            val dy = y - centerY
  2698	            if (dx * dx + dy * dy < innerRadiusSquared) {
  2699	                Color.TRANSPARENT
  2700	            } else {
  2701	                pixels[index]
  2702	            }
  2703	        }
  2704	        return Bitmap.createBitmap(bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight, Bitmap.Config.ARGB_8888).apply {
  2705	            setPixels(outputPixels, 0, bitmapWidth, 0, 0, bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight)
  2706	        }
  2707	    }
  2708	
  2709	    private fun createAvatarCharacterMask(
  2710	        pixels: IntArray,
  2711	        bitmapWidth: Int,
  2712	        bitmapHeight: Int,
  2713	        includeDarkSaturatedPixels: Boolean
  2714	    ): BooleanArray {
  2715	        val coloredPixels = BooleanArray(pixels.size)
  2716	        pixels.forEachIndexed { index, pixel ->
  2717	            coloredPixels[index] = isColorfulAvatarPixel(pixel, includeDarkSaturatedPixels)
  2718	        }
  2719	
  2720	        val mask = BooleanArray(pixels.size)
  2721	        val outlineRadius = 7
  2722	        for (y in 0 until bitmapHeight) {
  2723	            for (x in 0 until bitmapWidth) {
  2724	                val index = y * bitmapWidth + x
  2725	                if (!coloredPixels[index]) continue
  2726	
  2727	                mask[index] = true
  2728	                for (dy in -outlineRadius..outlineRadius) {
  2729	                    val sampleY = y + dy
  2730	                    if (sampleY !in 0 until bitmapHeight) continue
  2731	                    for (dx in -outlineRadius..outlineRadius) {
  2732	                        val sampleX = x + dx
  2733	                        if (sampleX !in 0 until bitmapWidth) continue
  2734	                        val sampleIndex = sampleY * bitmapWidth + sampleX
  2735	                        val samplePixel = pixels[sampleIndex]
  2736	                        val sampleAlpha = Color.alpha(samplePixel)
  2737	                        val sampleMax = maxOf(
  2738	                            Color.red(samplePixel),
  2739	                            Color.green(samplePixel),
  2740	                            Color.blue(samplePixel)
  2741	                        )
  2742	                        if (sampleAlpha > 16 && sampleMax <= 44) {
  2743	                            mask[sampleIndex] = true
  2744	                        }
  2745	                    }
  2746	                }
  2747	            }
  2748	        }
  2749	        return mask
  2750	    }
  2751	
  2752	    private fun isColorfulAvatarPixel(
  2753	        pixel: Int,
  2754	        includeDarkSaturatedPixels: Boolean
  2755	    ): Boolean {
  2756	        val alpha = Color.alpha(pixel)
  2757	        val red = Color.red(pixel)
  2758	        val green = Color.green(pixel)
  2759	        val blue = Color.blue(pixel)
  2760	        val channelMax = maxOf(red, green, blue)
  2761	        val channelMin = minOf(red, green, blue)
  2762	        if (alpha <= 16) return false
  2763	
  2764	        val chroma = channelMax - channelMin
  2765	        return (channelMax > 48 && chroma > 22) ||
  2766	            (includeDarkSaturatedPixels && channelMax > 36 && chroma > 14)
  2767	    }
  2768	
  2769	    private fun standardSignInBackgroundForSize(): Bitmap? {
  2770	        return when {
  2771	            isSizeCloseTo(width, STANDARD_TABLET_LONG_EDGE) &&
  2772	                isSizeCloseTo(height, STANDARD_TABLET_SHORT_EDGE) -> standardLandscapeBackground
  2773	            isSizeCloseTo(width, STANDARD_TABLET_SHORT_EDGE) &&
  2774	                isSizeCloseTo(height, STANDARD_TABLET_LONG_EDGE) -> standardPortraitBackground
  2775	            else -> null
  2776	        }
  2777	    }

  2780	        return abs(actual - expected) <= STANDARD_TABLET_SIZE_TOLERANCE
  2781	    }

  2787	    private fun settledAvatarHitProfile(x: Float, y: Float): CinerificProfile? {
  2788	        if (mockSignInStartMillis != null) return null
  2789	        if (!isFinalFrameSettled()) return null
  2790	
  2791	        val stage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return null
  2792	
  2793	        if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  2794	            val avatarSize = portraitAvatarSize()
  2795	            val centerX = stage.left + FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH * stage.scale / 2f
  2796	            val centerY = stage.top + (portraitAvatarTop() + avatarSize / 2f) * stage.scale
  2797	            val radius = avatarSize * stage.scale / 2f
  2798	            val dx = x - centerX
  2799	            val dy = y - centerY
  2800	            return if (dx * dx + dy * dy <= radius * radius) {
  2801	                FINAL_AVATAR_TARGETS[portraitProfileIndex].profile
  2802	            } else {
  2803	                null
  2804	            }
  2805	        }
  2806	
  2807	        val stackShiftY = signInStackShiftY()
  2808	        return FINAL_AVATAR_TARGETS.firstOrNull { target ->
  2809	            val bounds = target.bounds
  2810	            val centerX = stage.left + (bounds.x + bounds.w / 2f) * stage.scale
  2811	            val centerY = stage.top + (bounds.y + stackShiftY + bounds.h / 2f) * stage.scale
  2812	            val radius = min(bounds.w, bounds.h) * stage.scale / 2f
  2813	            val dx = x - centerX
  2814	            val dy = y - centerY
  2815	            dx * dx + dy * dy <= radius * radius
  2816	        }?.profile
  2817	    }

  3018	    private fun updateMockCreateAvatarDrag(event: MotionEvent): Boolean {
  3019	        if (!activeMockCreateAvatarDrag) return false

  3064	            val optionHeight = mockDropdownOptionHeight(dropdown)
  3065	            val top = stage.top + (yStart + optionIndex * optionHeight) * stage.scale
  3066	            val bottom = top + optionHeight * stage.scale

  3068	        }
  3069	        return null
  3070	    }

  3095	    private fun updateMockDropdownScroll(event: MotionEvent): Boolean {
  3096	        val dropdown = activeMockDropdownScroll ?: return false
  3097	        if (expandedMockDropdown != dropdown || !mockDropdownCanScroll(dropdown)) return false
  3098	
  3099	        val stage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return true
  3100	        val optionHeightPx = (mockDropdownOptionHeight(dropdown) * stage.scale).coerceAtLeast(1f)
  3101	        val dragDeltaY = event.y - mockDropdownScrollStartY
  3102	        if (abs(dragDeltaY) > touchSlop) {
  3103	            mockDropdownScrollMoved = true
  3104	            pressedMockDropdownOption = null
  3105	        }
  3106	
  3107	        if (mockDropdownScrollMoved) {
  3108	            val rowDelta = ((mockDropdownScrollStartY - event.y) / optionHeightPx).roundToInt()
  3109	            setMockDropdownScrollOffset(dropdown, mockDropdownScrollStartOffset + rowDelta)
  3110	            postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  3111	        }
  3112	        return true
  3113	    }

  3129	    private fun shouldStartMockDragReturn(event: MotionEvent): Boolean {
  3130	        if (mockDragReturnInProgress || isMockSignInClosing()) return false
  3131	
  3132	        val upwardDragY = mockTouchDownY - event.y
  3133	        if (upwardDragY <= mockBackDragThresholdPx()) return false
  3134	
  3135	        val dragX = event.x - mockTouchDownX
  3136	        return upwardDragY > abs(dragX)
  3137	    }

  3139	    private fun mockBackDragThresholdPx(): Float {
  3140	        val stageThreshold = currentStageMetrics()?.let { stage ->
  3141	            MOCK_BACK_DRAG_THRESHOLD * stage.scale
  3142	        } ?: MOCK_BACK_DRAG_THRESHOLD
  3143	        return maxOf(stageThreshold, touchSlop * 3f)
  3144	    }

  3164	        x: Float,
  3165	        y: Float,
  3166	        stage: StageMetrics
  3167	    ): Boolean {
  3168	        val left = stage.left + x * stage.scale
  3169	        val top = stage.top + (y - MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_HIT_TOP_PADDING) * stage.scale
  3170	        val right = stage.left + (x + MOCK_FORM_WIDTH) * stage.scale
  3171	        val bottom = stage.top + (y - MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_HIT_TOP_PADDING + MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_HIT_HEIGHT) * stage.scale
  3172	        return pointerX in left..right && pointerY in top..bottom
  3173	    }

  3199	        val left = stage.left + boxX * stage.scale
  3200	        val top = stage.top + selectorY * stage.scale

  3202	        val bottom = stage.top + (selectorY + MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SELECTOR_BOX_HEIGHT) * stage.scale
  3203	        return pointerX in left..right && pointerY in top..bottom

  3250	    private fun mockCreateAvatarCarouselBounds(
  3251	        columnX: Float,
  3252	        firstFieldY: Float,
  3253	        avatarBounds: Bounds
  3254	    ): Bounds {
  3255	        val centerX = columnX + MOCK_FORM_WIDTH / 2f
  3256	        val contentTop = firstFieldY
  3257	        val contentBottom = avatarBounds.y + avatarBounds.h
  3258	        val contentCenterY = (contentTop + contentBottom) / 2f
  3259	        val chevronSpan = avatarBounds.w +
  3260	            (MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CHEVRON_SIDE_GAP + MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CHEVRON_HIT_WIDTH / 2f) * 2f
  3261	        val side = maxOf(chevronSpan, contentBottom - contentTop)
  3262	        return Bounds(
  3263	            centerX - side / 2f,
  3264	            contentCenterY - side / 2f,
  3265	            side,
  3266	            side
  3267	        )
  3268	    }

  3289	    private fun mockCreateAvatarChevronContains(
  3290	        pointerX: Float,
  3291	        pointerY: Float,
  3292	        direction: MockAvatarCarouselDirection,
  3293	        avatarBounds: Bounds,
  3294	        stage: StageMetrics
  3295	    ): Boolean {
  3296	        val centerX = stage.left + mockCreateAvatarChevronCenterX(direction, avatarBounds) * stage.scale
  3297	        val centerY = stage.top + mockCreateAvatarChevronCenterY(avatarBounds) * stage.scale
  3298	        val halfWidth = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CHEVRON_HIT_WIDTH * stage.scale / 2f
  3299	        val halfHeight = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CHEVRON_HIT_HEIGHT * stage.scale / 2f
  3300	        return pointerX in (centerX - halfWidth)..(centerX + halfWidth) &&
  3301	            pointerY in (centerY - halfHeight)..(centerY + halfHeight)
  3302	    }

  3304	    private fun focusMockSignInField(field: MockSignInField) {
  3305	        mockInputDimAwaitingField = field
  3306	        mockInputDimActiveField = field
  3307	        mockInputDimActiveDropdown = null
  3308	        restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
  3309	        restartMockInputDimAnimation()
  3310	        if (focusedMockField != field) {
  3311	            activeComposingText = ""
  3312	            focusedMockField = field
  3313	            restartMockFormLabelAnimation()
  3314	            restartMockFormFocusAnimation()
  3315	        }
  3316	        requestFocus()
  3317	        post {
  3318	            val inputMethodManager = context.getSystemService(Context.INPUT_METHOD_SERVICE) as? InputMethodManager
  3319	            inputMethodManager?.restartInput(this)
  3320	            inputMethodManager?.showSoftInput(this, InputMethodManager.SHOW_IMPLICIT)
  3321	        }
  3322	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  3323	    }
  3324	
  3325	    private fun clearMockSignInFieldFocus() {
  3326	        if (focusedMockField == null) return
  3327	        activeComposingText = ""
  3328	        focusedMockField = null
  3329	        mockInputDimAwaitingField = null
  3330	        restartMockFormLabelAnimation()
  3331	        restartMockFormFocusAnimation()
  3332	        restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
  3333	        restartMockInputDimAnimation()
  3334	        val inputMethodManager = context.getSystemService(Context.INPUT_METHOD_SERVICE) as? InputMethodManager
  3335	        inputMethodManager?.hideSoftInputFromWindow(windowToken, 0)
  3336	        clearFocus()
  3337	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  3338	    }

  3563	    private fun mockDropdownVisibleOptionCount(dropdown: MockDropdown): Int {
  3564	        val optionCount = mockDropdownOptions(dropdown).size
  3565	        val viewportCount = if (mockDropdownUsesDateViewport(dropdown)) {
  3566	            MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_VISIBLE_OPTION_COUNT

  3573	    private fun mockDropdownCanScroll(dropdown: MockDropdown): Boolean {
  3574	        return mockDropdownOptions(dropdown).size > mockDropdownVisibleOptionCount(dropdown)
  3575	    }
  3576	
  3577	    private fun mockDropdownScrollOffset(dropdown: MockDropdown): Int {
  3578	        return (mockDropdownScrollOffsets[dropdown] ?: 0).coerceIn(0, mockDropdownMaxScrollOffset(dropdown))
  3579	    }
  3580	
  3581	    private fun mockDropdownMaxScrollOffset(dropdown: MockDropdown): Int {
  3582	        return (mockDropdownOptions(dropdown).size - mockDropdownVisibleOptionCount(dropdown)).coerceAtLeast(0)
  3583	    }
  3584	
  3585	    private fun setMockDropdownScrollOffset(dropdown: MockDropdown, offset: Int) {
  3586	        mockDropdownScrollOffsets[dropdown] = offset.coerceIn(0, mockDropdownMaxScrollOffset(dropdown))
  3587	    }
  3588	
  3589	    private fun mockDropdownVisibleOptions(dropdown: MockDropdown): List<String> {
  3590	        val options = mockDropdownOptions(dropdown)
  3591	        val offset = mockDropdownScrollOffset(dropdown)
  3592	        return options.drop(offset).take(mockDropdownVisibleOptionCount(dropdown))
  3593	    }
  3594	
  3595	    private fun prepareMockDropdownScroll(dropdown: MockDropdown) {
  3596	        if (!mockDropdownCanScroll(dropdown)) {
  3597	            setMockDropdownScrollOffset(dropdown, 0)
  3598	            return
  3599	        }
  3600	
  3601	        val selectedIndex = mockDropdownOptions(dropdown).indexOf(mockDropdownSelectedValue(dropdown))
  3602	        setMockDropdownScrollOffset(dropdown, if (selectedIndex >= 0) selectedIndex else 0)
  3603	    }

  3630	    private fun setMockDropdownValue(selection: MockDropdownOption) {
  3631	        mockInputDimAwaitingField = null
  3632	        mockInputDimActiveDropdown = selection.dropdown
  3633	        mockInputDimActiveField = null
  3634	        when (selection.dropdown) {
  3635	            MockDropdown.SubscriptionTier -> subscriptionTierText = normalizedMembershipTierSelection(selection.option)
  3636	            MockDropdown.Month -> monthText = selection.option
  3637	            MockDropdown.Day -> dayText = selection.option

  3740	    private fun mockForgotPasswordSubmissionProgress(): Float {
  3741	        val startMillis = forgotPasswordSubmissionStartMillis ?: return if (
  3742	            forgotPasswordSubmissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Sent
  3743	        ) {
  3744	            1f
  3745	        } else {
  3746	            0f
  3747	        }
  3748	        val elapsed = SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - startMillis
  3749	        return (elapsed / MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SUBMIT_MS.toFloat()).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  3750	    }

  3758	        if (forgotPasswordSubmissionState != MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Loading) {
  3759	            forgotPasswordSubmissionStartMillis = null
  3760	            return
  3761	        }
  3762	        if (SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - startMillis < MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SUBMIT_MS) return
  3763	
  3764	        forgotPasswordSubmissionState = MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Sent
  3765	        forgotPasswordSubmissionStartMillis = null

  3945	    private fun mockLandscapeInputLiftTarget(): Float {
  3946	        if (width <= height) return 0f
  3947	        if (mockSignInStartMillis == null || isMockSignInClosing()) return 0f
  3948	        return if (
  3949	            focusedMockField != null ||
  3950	            expandedMockDropdown != null ||
  3951	            isSoftKeyboardVisible()
  3952	        ) {
  3953	            1f
  3954	        } else {
  3955	            0f
  3956	        }
  3957	    }
  3958	
  3959	    private fun mockLandscapeInputStageLiftY(stageScale: Float): Float {
  3960	        if (width <= height || mockSignInStartMillis == null) return 0f
  3961	        val liftMotion = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockLandscapeInputLiftProgress)
  3962	        return MOCK_FORM_LANDSCAPE_FOCUS_SHIFT_Y * stageScale * liftMotion
  3963	    }
  3964	
  3965	    private fun isSoftKeyboardVisible(): Boolean {
  3966	        val rootInsets = ViewCompat.getRootWindowInsets(this)
  3967	        if (rootInsets != null) {
  3968	            val imeInsets = rootInsets.getInsets(WindowInsetsCompat.Type.ime())
  3969	            if (rootInsets.isVisible(WindowInsetsCompat.Type.ime()) || imeInsets.bottom > 0) return true
  3970	        }
  3971	
  3972	        val rootHeight = rootView?.height ?: 0
  3973	        if (rootHeight <= 0) return false
  3974	        getWindowVisibleDisplayFrame(tempWindowRect)
  3975	        val obscuredHeight = rootHeight - tempWindowRect.height()
  3976	        return obscuredHeight > rootHeight * 0.18f
  3977	    }

  3979	    private fun mockInputDimTarget(): Float {

  3981	        if (expandedMockDropdown != null) return 1f

  3983	        return if (focusedMockField == awaitingField) 1f else 0f
  3984	    }

  3990	        }
  3991	        if (activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount) return 0f
  3992	        return if (
  3993	            focusedMockField != null ||
  3994	            activeMockFields().any { mockSignInFieldText(it).isNotEmpty() }
  3995	        ) {
  3996	            1f
  3997	        } else {
  3998	            0f
  3999	        }
  4000	    }

  4088	    private fun appendMockSignInInput(rawInput: String): Boolean {
  4089	        val field = focusedMockField ?: return false
  4090	        val input = sanitizeMockSignInInput(rawInput)
  4091	        if (input.isEmpty()) return true
  4092	        val currentText = mockSignInFieldText(field)
  4093	        val baseText = currentText.dropLast(activeComposingText.length.coerceAtMost(currentText.length))
  4094	        val availableChars = (MOCK_FORM_INPUT_MAX_CHARS - baseText.length).coerceAtLeast(0)
  4095	        val insertedText = input.take(availableChars)
  4096	        setMockSignInFieldText(field, baseText + insertedText)
  4097	        activeComposingText = ""
  4098	        return true
  4099	    }
  4100	
  4101	    private fun replaceMockSignInComposingText(rawInput: String): Boolean {
  4102	        val field = focusedMockField ?: return false
  4103	        val input = sanitizeMockSignInInput(rawInput)
  4104	        val currentText = mockSignInFieldText(field)
  4105	        val baseText = currentText.dropLast(activeComposingText.length.coerceAtMost(currentText.length))
  4106	        val availableChars = (MOCK_FORM_INPUT_MAX_CHARS - baseText.length).coerceAtLeast(0)
  4107	        val composingText = input.take(availableChars)
  4108	        setMockSignInFieldText(field, baseText + composingText)
  4109	        activeComposingText = composingText
  4110	        return true
  4111	    }
  4112	
  4113	    private fun finishMockSignInComposingText(): Boolean {
  4114	        activeComposingText = ""
  4115	        return true
  4116	    }
  4117	
  4118	    private fun deleteMockSignInInput(beforeLength: Int): Boolean {
  4119	        val field = focusedMockField ?: return false
  4120	        val currentText = mockSignInFieldText(field)
  4121	        if (currentText.isEmpty()) {
  4122	            activeComposingText = ""
  4123	            return true
  4124	        }
  4125	        val deleteCount = beforeLength.coerceAtLeast(1).coerceAtMost(currentText.length)
  4126	        activeComposingText = ""
  4127	        setMockSignInFieldText(field, currentText.dropLast(deleteCount))
  4128	        return true
  4129	    }
  4130	
  4131	    private fun handleMockSignInEditorAction(actionCode: Int): Boolean {
  4132	        activeComposingText = ""
  4133	        val fields = activeMockFields()
  4134	        val fieldIndex = fields.indexOf(focusedMockField)
  4135	        if (fieldIndex >= 0 && fieldIndex < fields.lastIndex && actionCode != EditorInfo.IME_ACTION_DONE) {
  4136	            focusMockSignInField(fields[fieldIndex + 1])
  4137	        } else if (
  4138	            activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword &&
  4139	            focusedMockField == MockSignInField.Email &&
  4140	            mockForgotPasswordCanSubmit()
  4141	        ) {
  4142	            startMockForgotPasswordSubmission()
  4143	        } else {
  4144	            val inputMethodManager = context.getSystemService(Context.INPUT_METHOD_SERVICE) as? InputMethodManager
  4145	            inputMethodManager?.hideSoftInputFromWindow(windowToken, 0)
  4146	            clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
  4147	        }
  4148	        return true
  4149	    }
  4150	
  4151	    private fun handleMockSignInKeyEvent(keyCode: Int, event: KeyEvent): Boolean {
  4152	        if (event.action != KeyEvent.ACTION_DOWN) return true
  4153	        return when (keyCode) {
  4154	            KeyEvent.KEYCODE_DEL -> deleteMockSignInInput(1)
  4155	            KeyEvent.KEYCODE_ENTER -> handleMockSignInEditorAction(EditorInfo.IME_ACTION_DONE)
  4156	            else -> {
  4157	                val typedCharacter = event.unicodeChar.takeIf { it != 0 }?.toChar()?.toString()
  4158	                typedCharacter?.let(::appendMockSignInInput) ?: false
  4159	            }
  4160	        }
  4161	    }
  4162	
  4163	    private fun sanitizeMockSignInInput(input: String): String {
  4164	        return buildString {
  4165	            input.forEach { character ->
  4166	                if (character != '\n' && character != '\r' && !character.isISOControl()) {
  4167	                    append(character)
  4168	                }
  4169	            }
  4170	        }
  4171	    }
  4172	
  4173	    private fun trailingFittingText(text: String, maxWidth: Float, paint: Paint): String {
  4174	        if (paint.measureText(text) <= maxWidth) return text
  4175	
  4176	        var startIndex = 0
  4177	        while (startIndex < text.length && paint.measureText(text.substring(startIndex)) > maxWidth) {
  4178	            startIndex += 1
  4179	        }
  4180	        return text.substring(startIndex)
  4181	    }
  4182	
  4183	    private fun wrappedTextLines(text: String, maxWidth: Float, paint: Paint): List<String> {
  4184	        val lines = mutableListOf<String>()
  4185	        text.split(Regex("\\n\\s*\\n")).forEachIndexed { paragraphIndex, paragraph ->
  4186	            if (paragraphIndex > 0) {
  4187	                lines += ""
  4188	            }
  4189	
  4190	            var currentLine = ""
  4191	            paragraph.replace('\n', ' ')
  4192	                .split(Regex("\\s+"))
  4193	                .filter { it.isNotEmpty() }
  4194	                .forEach { word ->
  4195	                    val candidateLine = if (currentLine.isEmpty()) word else "$currentLine $word"
  4196	                    if (currentLine.isEmpty() || paint.measureText(candidateLine) <= maxWidth) {
  4197	                        currentLine = candidateLine
  4198	                    } else {
  4199	                        lines += currentLine
  4200	                        currentLine = word
  4201	                    }
  4202	                }
  4203	            if (currentLine.isNotEmpty()) {
  4204	                lines += currentLine
  4205	            }
  4206	        }
  4207	        return lines
  4208	    }
  4209	
  4210	    private fun moveToward(current: Float, target: Float, amount: Float): Float {
  4211	        return when {
  4212	            current < target -> (current + amount).coerceAtMost(target)
  4213	            current > target -> (current - amount).coerceAtLeast(target)
  4214	            else -> current
  4215	        }
  4216	    }
  4217	
  4218	    private fun currentStageMetrics(): StageMetrics? {
  4219	        if (width <= 0 || height <= 0) return null
  4220	
  4221	        val standardScale = min(width / FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH, height / FIGMA_FRAME_HEIGHT)
  4222	        val portraitScale = width / FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH
  4223	        val portraitAmount = portraitIntroAmount()
  4224	        val stageScale = lerpFloat(standardScale, portraitScale, portraitAmount)
  4225	        val standardTop = (height - FIGMA_FRAME_HEIGHT * standardScale) / 2f
  4226	        return StageMetrics(
  4227	            left = (width - FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH * stageScale) / 2f,
  4228	            top = lerpFloat(standardTop, 0f, portraitAmount) +
  4229	                mockAuthOpenStageLiftY(stageScale) +
  4230	                mockLandscapeInputStageLiftY(stageScale),
  4231	            scale = stageScale
  4232	        )
  4233	    }

  4236

  4238	        if (!isPortraitIntroLayout()) return 0f
  4239	        return 1f - FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockSignInProgress())
  4240	    }

  4253	        val top = portraitSafeTop()

  4255	    }

  4293	        val logoHeight = LOGO_FINAL_HEIGHT * PORTRAIT_INTRO_SCALE
  4294	        val rawLogoTop = portraitStackCenter(1f / 6f) - logoHeight / 2f
  4295	        val rawForgotCenter = portraitStackCenter(5f / 6f) + portraitPromptCenterSpacing()

  4297	        val bottomBreathingRoom = portraitSafeBottom() -
  4298	            (rawForgotCenter + PORTRAIT_PROMPT_VISIBLE_HALF_HEIGHT)

  4305	            (PORTRAIT_COMFORTABLE_DESIGN_HEIGHT - PORTRAIT_COMPACT_DESIGN_HEIGHT))
  4306	            .coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  4307	    }

  4379	    private fun switchMockAccountFlow(flow: MockAccountFlow) {
  4380	        if (
  4381	            mockSignInStartMillis == null ||
  4382	            activeMockFlow == flow ||
  4383	            isMockSignInClosing() ||
  4384	            isMockFlowSwitchAnimating()
  4385	        ) {
  4386	            return
  4387	        }
  4388	
  4389	        val previousFlow = activeMockFlow ?: return
  4390	        clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
  4391	        activeComposingText = ""
  4392	        expandedMockDropdown = null
  4393	        activeMockDropdownScroll = null
  4394	        mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
  4395	        pressedMockField = null
  4396	        pressedRememberMe = false
  4397	        pressedBackChevron = false
  4398	        pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
  4399	        activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = false
  4400	        mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
  4401	        mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX = 0f
  4402	        pressedMockDropdown = null
  4403	        pressedMockDropdownOption = null
  4404	        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = null
  4405	        pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = false
  4406	        resetMockCreateAvatarCarousel()
  4407	        if (flow == MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword) {
  4408	            forgotRecoveryTarget = MockForgotRecoveryTarget.Password
  4409	            emailText = ""
  4410	            resetMockForgotPasswordSubmission()
  4411	        }
  4412	        outgoingMockFlow = previousFlow
  4413	        activeMockFlow = flow
  4414	        mockFlowSwitchStartMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
  4415	        restartMockFormLabelAnimation()
  4416	        restartMockFormFocusAnimation()
  4417	        restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
  4418	        restartMockInputDimAnimation()
  4419	        notifyIntroSnapshotChanged()
  4420	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  4421	    }

  4506	    private fun mockSignInProgress(): Float {
  4507	        val startMillis = mockSignInStartMillis ?: return 0f
  4508	        val elapsed = SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - startMillis
  4509	        val progress = (elapsed / mockSignInTransitionDurationMs().toFloat()).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  4510	        return lerpFloat(
  4511	            mockSignInTransitionStartProgress,
  4512	            mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress,
  4513	            progress
  4514	        )
  4515	    }
  4516	
  4517	    private fun mockFlowSwitchProgress(): Float {
  4518	        val startMillis = mockFlowSwitchStartMillis ?: return 1f
  4519	        return ((SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - startMillis) / MOCK_SIGN_IN_TRANSITION_MS.toFloat())
  4520	            .coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  4521	    }

  4523	    private fun isMockFlowSwitchAnimating(): Boolean {
  4524	        return mockFlowSwitchStartMillis != null && mockFlowSwitchProgress() < 1f
  4525	    }

  4528	        if (mockFlowSwitchStartMillis == null || mockFlowSwitchProgress() < 1f) return

  4530	        mockFlowSwitchStartMillis = null
  4531	    }

  4533	    private fun isMockSignInAnimating(): Boolean {
  4534	        val startMillis = mockSignInStartMillis ?: return false
  4535	        return SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - startMillis < mockSignInTransitionDurationMs()
  4536	    }
  4537	
  4538	    private fun isMockSignInClosing(): Boolean {
  4539	        return mockSignInStartMillis != null && mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress == 0f
  4540	    }
  4541	
  4542	    private fun mockSignInTransitionDurationMs(): Long {
  4543	        val distance = abs(mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress - mockSignInTransitionStartProgress)
  4544	        return (MOCK_SIGN_IN_TRANSITION_MS * distance)
  4545	            .roundToInt()
  4546	            .coerceAtLeast(1)
  4547	            .toLong()
  4548	    }
  4549	
  4550	    private fun finishMockSignInTransitionIfNeeded() {
  4551	        val startMillis = mockSignInStartMillis ?: return
  4552	        if (SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - startMillis < mockSignInTransitionDurationMs()) return
  4553	
  4554	        if (mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress == 0f) {
  4555	            mockSignInStartMillis = null
  4556	            activeMockFlow = null
  4557	            mockSignInTransitionStartProgress = 0f
  4558	            mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress = 0f
  4559	            pressedMockField = null
  4560	            pressedRememberMe = false
  4561	            pressedBackChevron = false
  4562	            pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
  4563	            pressedMockDropdown = null
  4564	            pressedMockDropdownOption = null
  4565	            pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = null
  4566	            pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = false
  4567	            activeMockDropdownScroll = null
  4568	            mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
  4569	            mockDragReturnInProgress = false
  4570	            resetMockForgotPasswordSubmission()
  4571	            resetMockCreateAvatarCarousel()
  4572	            notifyIntroSnapshotChanged()
  4573	            return
  4574	        }
  4575	
  4576	        mockSignInTransitionStartProgress = 1f
  4577	        mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress = 1f
  4578	    }

  4601	        override fun getTextBeforeCursor(n: Int, flags: Int): CharSequence {
  4602	            val text = focusedMockField?.let(::mockSignInFieldText).orEmpty()

  4607	            return ""

  4616	        }
  4617	
  4618	        override fun sendKeyEvent(event: KeyEvent): Boolean {
  4619	            if (event.action != KeyEvent.ACTION_DOWN) return true
  4620	            return when (event.keyCode) {
  4621	                KeyEvent.KEYCODE_DEL -> deleteMockSignInInput(1)
  4622	                KeyEvent.KEYCODE_ENTER -> handleMockSignInEditorAction(EditorInfo.IME_ACTION_DONE)
  4623	                else -> {
  4624	                    val typedCharacter = event.unicodeChar.takeIf { it != 0 }?.toChar()?.toString()
  4625	                    if (typedCharacter != null) {
  4626	                        appendMockSignInInput(typedCharacter)
  4627	                    } else {
  4628	                        super.sendKeyEvent(event)
  4629	                    }
  4630	                }
  4631	            }
  4632	        }
  4633	    }

  4773	    val profile: CinerificProfile,
  4774	    val bounds: Bounds

  4778	    val left: Float,
  4779	    val top: Float,
  4780	    val scale: Float
  4781	)
  4782	
  4783	private data class Bounds(
  4784	    val x: Float,
  4785	    val y: Float,
  4786	    val w: Float,
  4787	    val h: Float
  4788	)
  4789	
  4790	private inline fun <reified T : Enum<T>> enumValueOrNull(name: String): T? {
  4791	    return runCatching { enumValueOf<T>(name) }.getOrNull()
  4792	}
  4793	
  4794	private fun bootProgressAt(bootStartMillis: Long): Float {
  4795	    val elapsed = SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - bootStartMillis
  4796	    return (elapsed / BOOT_ANIMATION_MS.toFloat()).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  4797	}
  4798	
  4799	private fun linearSegmentMs(progress: Float, startMs: Int, endMs: Int): Float {
  4800	    val ms = progress.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * BOOT_ANIMATION_MS
  4801	    return ((ms - startMs) / (endMs - startMs).toFloat()).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  4802	}
  4803	
  4804	private fun easedSegmentMs(progress: Float, startMs: Int, endMs: Int): Float {
  4805	    return FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(linearSegmentMs(progress, startMs, endMs))
  4806	}
  4807	
  4808	private fun bouncySegmentMs(progress: Float, startMs: Int, endMs: Int): Float {
  4809	    return bouncyEasing(linearSegmentMs(progress, startMs, endMs))
  4810	}
  4811	
  4812	private fun bouncyEasing(value: Float): Float {
  4813	    val t = value.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  4814	    val base = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(t)
  4815	    val bounce = sin(t * PI.toFloat() * 4.5f) * (1f - t) * 0.13f
  4816	    return base + bounce
  4817	}
  4818	
  4819	private fun lerpFloat(start: Float, end: Float, amount: Float) = start + (end - start) * amount
  4820	
  4821	private fun lerpBounds(start: Bounds, end: Bounds, amount: Float) = Bounds(
  4822	    x = lerpFloat(start.x, end.x, amount),
  4823	    y = lerpFloat(start.y, end.y, amount),
  4824	    w = lerpFloat(start.w, end.w, amount),
  4825	    h = lerpFloat(start.h, end.h, amount)
  4826	)
  4827	
  4828	private fun offsetBounds(bounds: Bounds, yOffset: Float) = Bounds(
  4829	    x = bounds.x,
  4830	    y = bounds.y + yOffset,
  4831	    w = bounds.w,
  4832	    h = bounds.h
  4833	)
  4834	
  4835	private fun lerpColor(start: Int, end: Int, amount: Float): Int {
  4836	    val t = amount.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  4837	    return Color.argb(
  4838	        lerpInt(Color.alpha(start), Color.alpha(end), t),
  4839	        lerpInt(Color.red(start), Color.red(end), t),
  4840	        lerpInt(Color.green(start), Color.green(end), t),
  4841	        lerpInt(Color.blue(start), Color.blue(end), t)
  4842	    )
  4843	}
  4844	
  4845	private fun lerpInt(start: Int, end: Int, amount: Float): Int {
  4846	    return (start + (end - start) * amount).roundToInt()
  4847	}
  4848	
  4849	private fun combineBitmaps(base: Bitmap, overlay: Bitmap): Bitmap {
  4850	    val output = Bitmap.createBitmap(base.width, base.height, Bitmap.Config.ARGB_8888)
  4851	    val canvas = Canvas(output)
  4852	    val paint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG or Paint.FILTER_BITMAP_FLAG or Paint.DITHER_FLAG)
  4853	    canvas.drawBitmap(base, 0f, 0f, paint)
  4854	    canvas.drawBitmap(overlay, 0f, 0f, paint)
  4855	    return output
  4856	}
  4857	
  4858	private fun circularBitmap(source: Bitmap): Bitmap {
  4859	    val output = Bitmap.createBitmap(source.width, source.height, Bitmap.Config.ARGB_8888)
  4860	    val canvas = Canvas(output)
  4861	    val rect = RectF(0f, 0f, source.width.toFloat(), source.height.toFloat())
  4862	    val maskPaint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG)
  4863	    val imagePaint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG or Paint.FILTER_BITMAP_FLAG or Paint.DITHER_FLAG)
  4864	    canvas.drawOval(rect, maskPaint)
  4865	    imagePaint.xfermode = PorterDuffXfermode(PorterDuff.Mode.SRC_IN)
  4866	    canvas.drawBitmap(source, null, rect, imagePaint)
  4867	    imagePaint.xfermode = null
  4868	    return output
  4869	}

    32	}

============================================================
FILE: README.md
============================================================

     1	# Cinerific
     2	
     3	Local Android project for the `prahlin/cinerific` GitHub repo.
     4	
     5	This is a first Kotlin / Jetpack Compose scaffold based on the Figma intro and sign-in frames. The current app opens as a tablet-friendly full-screen frame preview with:
     6	
     7	- Intro frame 1
     8	- Animated intro frame 2
     9	- Promo/logo intro frame 3
    10	- Sign-in/loading frame 4
    11	
    12	## Stack
    13	
    14	- Kotlin
    15	- Jetpack Compose Material 3
    16	- Android Gradle Plugin 8.5.x
    17	- Gradle 8.7
    18	
    19	## Run it
    20	
    21	Open the project in Android Studio and run `app`, or use the Gradle wrapper:
    22	
    23	```bash
    24	./gradlew :app:assembleDebug
    25	```
    26	
    27	The project pins Gradle to Android Studio's bundled JBR in `gradle.properties`, so command-line builds do not accidentally use an unsupported system JDK.
    28	
    29	From VS Code, run the default build task `Android: assemble debug`. There are also tasks for `Android: install debug` and `Android: launch Cinerific` when an emulator or device is connected.
    30	
    31	### Standard tablet emulator
    32	
    33	Use the guarded launcher for the persistent low-load QA profile:
    34	
    35	```bash
    36	./start_cinerific_android_standard.sh
    37	```
    38	
    39	The launcher requests and verifies `1280x800` at `160 dpi`, two guest CPU cores,
    40	host GPU acceleration, and the repository-owned rounded tablet bezel before
    41	opening Cinerific. It refuses to continue if the running AVD does not match.
    42	Emulator logs are written to
    43	`~/Library/Logs/Cinerific/standard-emulator.log`.
    44	
    45	## Notes for the first Kotlin pass
    46	
    47	- The layout is intentionally simple and adaptive for tablets.
    48	- The current visuals are Compose approximations of the Figma frames and are meant to be replaced with production assets screen by screen.
    49	- The local project folder is the same path as the GitHub remote: `prahlin/cinerific`.

============================================================
FILE: start_cinerific_android_standard.sh
============================================================

     1	#!/usr/bin/env bash
     2	set -Eeuo pipefail
     3	
     4	ROOT="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"
     5	SDK="${ANDROID_HOME:-${ANDROID_SDK_ROOT:-$HOME/Library/Android/sdk}}"
     6	EMU="${SDK}/emulator/emulator"
     7	ADB="${SDK}/platform-tools/adb"
     8	AVD="Cinerific_Standard_Tablet"
     9	AVD_CONFIG="$HOME/.android/avd/${AVD}.avd/config.ini"
    10	DEVICE="emulator-5554"
    11	PORT="5554"
    12	APP_PACKAGE="com.prahlin.cinerific"
    13	APP_ACTIVITY="${APP_PACKAGE}/.MainActivity"
    14	EXPECTED_SIZE="1280x800"
    15	EXPECTED_DENSITY="160"
    16	EXPECTED_CORES="2"
    17	EXPECTED_SKIN="cinerific_pixel_tablet_1280"
    18	BOOT_TIMEOUT_SECONDS="${BOOT_TIMEOUT_SECONDS:-180}"
    19	LOG_DIR="$HOME/Library/Logs/Cinerific"
    20	LOG="$LOG_DIR/standard-emulator.log"
    21	ERROR_LOG="$LOG_DIR/standard-emulator.err.log"
    22	LAUNCHD_LABEL="com.cinerific.standardavd"
    23	LAUNCHD_PLIST="$ROOT/launchd/${LAUNCHD_LABEL}.plist"
    24	
    25	fail() {
    26	  printf 'ERROR: %s\n' "$*" >&2
    27	  exit 1
    28	}
    29	
    30	[[ -x "$EMU" ]] || fail "Android emulator not found at $EMU"
    31	[[ -x "$ADB" ]] || fail "adb not found at $ADB"
    32	[[ -f "$LAUNCHD_PLIST" ]] || fail "LaunchAgent not found at $LAUNCHD_PLIST"
    33	[[ -f "$AVD_CONFIG" ]] || fail "AVD configuration not found at $AVD_CONFIG"
    34	"$EMU" -list-avds | grep -qx "$AVD" || fail "AVD $AVD was not found"
    35	
    36	config_skin="$(sed -n 's/^skin.name=//p' "$AVD_CONFIG")"
    37	config_frame="$(sed -n 's/^showDeviceFrame=//p' "$AVD_CONFIG")"
    38	[[ "$config_skin" == "$EXPECTED_SKIN" ]] || fail "Expected skin $EXPECTED_SKIN, got $config_skin"
    39	[[ "$config_frame" == "yes" ]] || fail "Device frame is not enabled in $AVD_CONFIG"
    40	
    41	mkdir -p "$LOG_DIR"
    42	
    43	device_is_connected() {
    44	  "$ADB" devices | awk -v device="$DEVICE" \
    45	    '$1 == device && $2 == "device" { found = 1 } END { exit !found }'
    46	}
    47	
    48	if device_is_connected; then
    49	  actual_avd="$("$ADB" -s "$DEVICE" emu avd name 2>/dev/null | tr -d '\r' | sed -n '1p')"
    50	  [[ "$actual_avd" == "$AVD" ]] || fail "$DEVICE is running $actual_avd, not $AVD"
    51	  printf '%s is already running.\n' "$AVD"
    52	else
    53	  printf 'Starting %s with the low-load QA profile...\n' "$AVD"
    54	  launchctl bootout "gui/$(id -u)/${LAUNCHD_LABEL}" >/dev/null 2>&1 || true
    55	  : > "$LOG"
    56	  : > "$ERROR_LOG"
    57	  launchctl bootstrap "gui/$(id -u)" "$LAUNCHD_PLIST"
    58	fi
    59	
    60	deadline=$((SECONDS + BOOT_TIMEOUT_SECONDS))
    61	until device_is_connected; do
    62	  (( SECONDS < deadline )) || fail "Emulator did not connect within ${BOOT_TIMEOUT_SECONDS}s; see $LOG"
    63	  sleep 2
    64	done
    65	
    66	until [[ "$("$ADB" -s "$DEVICE" shell getprop sys.boot_completed 2>/dev/null | tr -d '\r')" == "1" ]]; do
    67	  (( SECONDS < deadline )) || fail "Android did not finish booting within ${BOOT_TIMEOUT_SECONDS}s; see $LOG"
    68	  sleep 2
    69	done
    70	
    71	actual_avd="$("$ADB" -s "$DEVICE" emu avd name 2>/dev/null | tr -d '\r' | sed -n '1p')"
    72	actual_size="$("$ADB" -s "$DEVICE" shell wm size | sed -n 's/^Physical size: //p' | tr -d '\r')"
    73	actual_density="$("$ADB" -s "$DEVICE" shell wm density | sed -n 's/^Physical density: //p' | tr -d '\r')"
    74	actual_cores="$("$ADB" -s "$DEVICE" shell 'grep -c "^processor" /proc/cpuinfo' | tr -d '\r')"
    75	
    76	[[ "$actual_avd" == "$AVD" ]] || fail "Expected $AVD, got $actual_avd"
    77	[[ "$actual_size" == "$EXPECTED_SIZE" ]] || fail "Expected $EXPECTED_SIZE, got $actual_size"
    78	[[ "$actual_density" == "$EXPECTED_DENSITY" ]] || fail "Expected density $EXPECTED_DENSITY, got $actual_density"
    79	[[ "$actual_cores" == "$EXPECTED_CORES" ]] || fail "Expected $EXPECTED_CORES cores, got $actual_cores"
    80	
    81	sleep 10
    82	device_is_connected || fail "Emulator disconnected after boot; see $LOG"
    83	
    84	if "$ADB" -s "$DEVICE" shell pm path "$APP_PACKAGE" >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    85	  "$ADB" -s "$DEVICE" shell am force-stop "$APP_PACKAGE"
    86	  "$ADB" -s "$DEVICE" shell am start -n "$APP_ACTIVITY" >/dev/null
    87	  printf 'Cinerific launched on %s.\n' "$DEVICE"
    88	else
    89	  printf 'Cinerific is not installed on %s; emulator verification still passed.\n' "$DEVICE"
    90	fi
    91	
    92	printf 'Verified: %s @ %s dpi, %s cores, host GPU.\n' \
    93	  "$actual_size" "$actual_density" "$actual_cores"
    94	printf 'Emulator log: %s\n' "$LOG"


============================================================
FILE: read_project.sh
============================================================

     1	#!/bin/bash
     2	
     3	PROJECT_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "$0")" && pwd)"
     4	OUTPUT_FILE="$PROJECT_DIR/cinerific_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt"
     5	
     6	echo "Generating Cinerific project snapshot..."
     7	echo "Project directory: $PROJECT_DIR"
     8	echo "Snapshot: $OUTPUT_FILE"
     9	echo ""
    10	
    11	write_header() {
    12	  cat > "$OUTPUT_FILE" <<EOF
    13	Cinerific Android PROJECT SNAPSHOT
    14	Generated: $(date)
    15	Project directory: $PROJECT_DIR
    16	
    17	IMPORTANT INSTRUCTIONS FOR CHATGPT & CODEX:
    18	1. Read this entire snapshot before giving code edits.
    19	2. Preserve all assets, spacing, alignment, fonts, images, layout, animations, and relative positioning unless specifically asked to change them.
    20	3. Do not remove unrelated code.
    21	4. Do not rewrite large sections unless requested.
    22	5. When giving code changes, include exact line/row numbers whenever possible.
    23	6. Print full scripts when requested.
    24	7. Treat this project structure as the source of truth.
    25	8. If you don't understand something, ask for clarification instead of making assumptions.
    26	9. Warn me, or at least caution me, if I ask for something that would break the project or cause it to not work as intended.
    27	
    28	
    29	============================================================
    30	SNAPSHOT IGNORE POLICY
    31	============================================================
    32	
    33	This file is rebuilt from the current Cinerific workspace every time ./read_project.sh runs.
    34	Ignored paths are omitted from the project tree and file dumps unless they appear inside an included text file such as .gitignore or read_project.sh.
    35	
    36	The snapshot intentionally ignores:
    37	- .git
    38	- .gradle
    39	- .idea
    40	- app/build
    41	- build
    42	- .kotlin
    43	- .tmp
    44	- .cache
    45	- .DS_Store
    46	- local.properties
    47	- *_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt
    48	- *.jks
    49	- *.keystore
    50	
    51	Binary image and screenshot files are inventoried by path, size, and modified time only. Raw binary bytes are not printed.
    52	
    53	
    54	============================================================
    55	PROJECT FILE TREE
    56	============================================================
    57	
    58	EOF
    59	}
    60	
    61	print_file() {
    62	  local file="$1"
    63	
    64	  if [ -f "$file" ]; then
    65	    {
    66	      echo ""
    67	      echo ""
    68	      echo "============================================================"
    69	      echo "FILE: ${file#$PROJECT_DIR/}"
    70	      echo "============================================================"
    71	      echo ""
    72	      nl -ba "$file"
    73	    } >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
    74	  fi
    75	}
    76	
    77	print_binary_inventory() {
    78	  local title="$1"
    79	  local dir="$2"
    80	
    81	  {
    82	    echo ""
    83	    echo ""
    84	    echo "============================================================"
    85	    echo "$title"
    86	    echo "============================================================"
    87	    echo ""
    88	  } >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
    89	
    90	  if [ ! -d "$dir" ]; then
    91	    echo "Directory not present: ${dir#$PROJECT_DIR/}" >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
    92	    return
    93	  fi
    94	
    95	  find "$dir" -type f \
    96	    ! -name ".DS_Store" \
    97	    ! -name "*_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt" \
    98	    ! -name "*.jks" \
    99	    ! -name "*.keystore" \
   100	    | sort | while IFS= read -r file; do
   101	    local size
   102	    local modified
   103	    size="$(stat -f "%z" "$file" 2>/dev/null || stat -c "%s" "$file" 2>/dev/null || echo "unknown")"
   104	    modified="$(stat -f "%Sm" -t "%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S" "$file" 2>/dev/null || stat -c "%y" "$file" 2>/dev/null || echo "unknown")"
   105	    printf '%s | %s bytes | modified %s\n' "${file#$PROJECT_DIR/}" "$size" "$modified" >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
   106	  done
   107	}
   108	
   109	write_snapshot() {
   110	  write_header
   111	
   112	  find "$PROJECT_DIR" \
   113	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.git" -prune -o \
   114	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.gradle" -prune -o \
   115	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.idea" -prune -o \
   116	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/app/build" -prune -o \
   117	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/build" -prune -o \
   118	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.kotlin" -prune -o \
   119	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.tmp" -prune -o \
   120	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.cache" -prune -o \
   121	    -name ".DS_Store" -prune -o \
   122	    -name "local.properties" -prune -o \
   123	    -name "*_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt" -prune -o \
   124	    -name "*.jks" -prune -o \
   125	    -name "*.keystore" -prune -o \
   126	    -print | sort >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
   127	
   128	  cat >> "$OUTPUT_FILE" <<EOF
   129	
   130	
   131	============================================================
   132	FILE CONTENTS — ANDROID SOURCE
   133	============================================================
   134	
   135	EOF
   136	
   137	  for file in \
   138	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/AndroidManifest.xml" \
   139	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/java/com/prahlin/cinerific/MainActivity.kt" \
   140	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/java/com/prahlin/cinerific/ui/CinerificApp.kt" \
   141	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/java/com/prahlin/cinerific/ui/CinerificIntroView.kt" \
   142	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/java/com/prahlin/cinerific/ui/theme/Theme.kt" \

NOT INCLUDED IN SNAPSHOT
============================================================

- .git
- .gradle
- .idea
- app/build
- build
- .kotlin
- .tmp
- .cache
- .DS_Store
- local.properties
- *_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt
- *.jks
- *.keystore

The snapshot lists binary assets and screenshots by path, size, and modified time, but does not print raw PNG/JPG/PDF bytes.
